===== BEGIN FILE apps/web/lib/marker-pool.ts bytes=14436 sha256=d614170bbb7138121f82fe8511d0e60d8dbe786e5bfc4d1e6dfb95c290b02e6c =====
/**
 * 마커 풀링 (Object Pool) 시스템
 * 
 * 마커 객체를 재사용하여 생성/삭제 오버헤드를 제거하고
 * 가비지 컬렉션 부담을 현저히 감소시킵니다.
 * 
 * @example
 * const pool = MarkerPool.getInstance();
 * const marker = pool.acquire('restaurant-123', position, icon);
 * // ... 사용 후
 * pool.release('restaurant-123');
 */

import { syncRetainedMarkerAccessibility } from './retained-marker-accessibility';
import { detachNaverMapMarker, removeNaverMapListener } from './naver-map-event-cleanup';

type MarkerClickEvent = unknown;

interface MarkerAnchorLike {
    x: number;
    y: number;
}

interface MarkerIconLike {
    content?: unknown;
    anchor?: MarkerAnchorLike | null;
    [key: string]: unknown;
}

interface MarkerPositionLike {
    equals?: (position: unknown) => boolean;
    lat?: () => number;
    lng?: () => number;
}

const MARKER_NODE_SELECTOR = '[data-testid="marker"]';
const REVIEW_BUBBLE_SELECTOR = '[data-visible-marker-review-bubble="true"]';

function patchStableMarkerReviewBubble(element: HTMLElement, previousContent: unknown, nextContent: unknown): boolean {
    if (typeof previousContent !== 'string' || typeof nextContent !== 'string' || typeof document === 'undefined') return false;
    if (!nextContent.includes('data-testid="marker"') && !element.querySelector(MARKER_NODE_SELECTOR)) {
        return false;
    }

    const currentMarker = element.querySelector(MARKER_NODE_SELECTOR);
    if (!currentMarker) return false;

    const template = document.createElement('template');
    template.innerHTML = nextContent;
    const nextMarker = template.content.querySelector(MARKER_NODE_SELECTOR);
    if (!nextMarker) return false;
    if (currentMarker.getAttribute('style') !== nextMarker.getAttribute('style')) return false;
    // A pooled SDK object may now represent a different restaurant. Keeping its
    // image is safe only when the marker identity and fixed badge are unchanged.
    for (const attribute of ['data-restaurant-id', 'role', 'tabindex', 'aria-label', 'title']) {
        if (currentMarker.getAttribute(attribute) !== nextMarker.getAttribute(attribute)) return false;
    }
    const visitBadgeSelector = '[data-tzuyang-visit-count-badge="true"]';
    if ((currentMarker.querySelector(visitBadgeSelector)?.outerHTML ?? '')
        !== (nextMarker.querySelector(visitBadgeSelector)?.outerHTML ?? '')) return false;


    const currentImage = currentMarker.querySelector('img');
    const nextImage = nextMarker.querySelector('img');
    if ((currentImage?.getAttribute('src') ?? '') !== (nextImage?.getAttribute('src') ?? '')) return false;

    // Only the review bubble may differ. A partial attribute comparison misses
    // changes outside the image node, including the popularity tier and flame.
    const previous = document.createElement('template');
    previous.innerHTML = previousContent;
    const nextStatic = template.content.cloneNode(true) as DocumentFragment;
    for (const content of [previous.content, nextStatic]) {
        content.querySelectorAll(REVIEW_BUBBLE_SELECTOR).forEach(node => node.remove());
    }
    if (!previous.content.isEqualNode(nextStatic)) return false;

    // The real Naver wrapper keeps the bubble outside the popularity/category
    // wrappers. Replace it at its existing anchor, rather than inserting a
    // second bubble inside the image marker. Structural add/remove uses setIcon.
    const currentBubbles = element.querySelectorAll(REVIEW_BUBBLE_SELECTOR);
    const nextBubbles = template.content.querySelectorAll(REVIEW_BUBBLE_SELECTOR);
    if (currentBubbles.length !== 1 || nextBubbles.length !== 1) return false;
    // Keep the current viewport clamp until the next layout pass recalculates it.
    if (currentBubbles[0] instanceof HTMLElement && nextBubbles[0] instanceof HTMLElement) {
        nextBubbles[0].style.marginLeft = currentBubbles[0].style.marginLeft;
    }
    currentBubbles[0].replaceWith(nextBubbles[0]);
    return true;
}

interface PooledMarker {
    __tzudongRetainedMarkerAriaHidden?: boolean;
    __onClick?: (event: MarkerClickEvent) => void;
    __disposeClickListener?: () => void;
    getMap: () => unknown;
    setMap: (map: unknown | null) => void;
    getPosition: () => MarkerPositionLike | null;
    setPosition: (position: unknown) => void;
    getIcon: () => MarkerIconLike | null;
    setIcon: (icon: MarkerIconLike) => void;
    getElement: () => HTMLElement | null;
    setZIndex: (zIndex: number) => void;
}

/**
 * 싱글톤 마커 풀 클래스
 */
export class MarkerPool {
    private static instance: MarkerPool | null = null;

    /** 사용 가능한 마커 풀 */
    private pool: PooledMarker[] = [];

    /** 현재 활성화된 마커 맵 (ID → Marker) */
    private active: Map<string, PooledMarker> = new Map();

    // The SDK may return a cloned descriptor. The pool owns the last applied
    // snapshot; review patches must not depend on mutating getIcon() results.
    private renderedIcons = new WeakMap<PooledMarker, MarkerIconLike>();

    /** 풀 최대 크기 (메모리 제한) */
    private readonly MAX_POOL_SIZE = 1000;

    /** 통계 (디버깅용) */
    private stats = {
        created: 0,
        reused: 0,
        released: 0,
    };

    private constructor() {
        // 싱글톤 패턴
    }

    /**
     * 싱글톤 인스턴스 가져오기
     */
    public static getInstance(): MarkerPool {
        if (!MarkerPool.instance) {
            MarkerPool.instance = new MarkerPool();
        }
        return MarkerPool.instance;
    }

    /**
     * 마커 획득 (풀에서 재사용 or 새로 생성)
     * 
     * @param id 마커 고유 ID (레스토랑 ID 등)
     * @param position 마커 위치
     * @param icon 마커 아이콘
     * @param map 지도 인스턴스
     * @returns 마커 객체
     */
    public acquire(
        id: string,
        position: unknown,
        icon: MarkerIconLike,
        map: unknown,
        onClick?: () => void
    ): PooledMarker {
        let marker: PooledMarker;
        let isNew = false;

        // 1. 이미 활성화된 마커 재사용
        if (this.active.has(id)) {
            marker = this.active.get(id)!;
        }
        else if (this.pool.length > 0) {
            marker = this.pool.pop()!;
            this.stats.reused++;
        }
        // 3. 새 마커 생성
        else {
            marker = new window.naver.maps.Marker({
                position, // 초기값
                icon,     // 초기값
                map,      // 초기값
            }) as PooledMarker;
            isNew = true;
            this.stats.created++;

            // [PERFORMANCE] 이벤트 위임: 마커 생성 시 단 1회만 리스너 등록
            // 이후 핸들러 교체는 __onClick 프로퍼티만 변경하여 zero-overhead 달성
            const events = window.naver.maps.Event;
            const listener = events.addListener(marker, 'click', (e: MarkerClickEvent) => {
                if (marker.__onClick) {
                    marker.__onClick(e);
                }
            });
            if (listener) marker.__disposeClickListener = () => { removeNaverMapListener(events, listener); };
        }

        // [PERFORMANCE] 불필요한 DOM 조작/렌더링 방지
        // 값이 실제로 변경되었을 때만 setter 호출

        // 1. Map 설정 (새로 생성된 경우 이미 설정됨)
        if (!isNew && marker.getMap() !== map) {
            marker.setMap(map);
        }

        // 2. 위치 업데이트 (새로 생성된 경우 이미 설정됨)
        if (!isNew) {
            const currentPos = marker.getPosition();
            // LatLng.equals 메서드 활용 또는 좌표값 비교
            if (!currentPos?.equals || !currentPos.equals(position)) {
                marker.setPosition(position);
            }
        }

        // 3. Keep all pooled icon writers behind the same rendered snapshot.
        if (isNew) {
            this.renderedIcons.set(marker, { ...icon, anchor: icon.anchor ? { ...icon.anchor } : icon.anchor });
        } else {
            this.applyIcon(marker, icon);
        }

        // 4. 이벤트 핸들러 교체 (프로퍼티 할당 O(1))
        marker.__onClick = onClick;

        this.active.set(id, marker);
        return marker;
    }

    private applyIcon(marker: PooledMarker, icon: MarkerIconLike): void {
        const currentIcon = this.renderedIcons.get(marker) ?? marker.getIcon();
        const currentAnchor = currentIcon?.anchor;
        const nextAnchor = icon.anchor;
        const anchorChanged = (currentAnchor?.x ?? null) !== (nextAnchor?.x ?? null)
            || (currentAnchor?.y ?? null) !== (nextAnchor?.y ?? null);
        if (!anchorChanged && currentIcon?.content === icon.content) return;

        const element = marker.getElement();
        const patched = !anchorChanged && typeof element?.querySelector === 'function'
            && patchStableMarkerReviewBubble(element, currentIcon?.content, icon.content);
        if (!patched) {
            const wasHidden = Boolean(marker.__tzudongRetainedMarkerAriaHidden);
            // Only SDK replacement changes the root. An in-place review patch
            // keeps its existing accessibility owner and hidden state intact.
            syncRetainedMarkerAccessibility(marker, true);
            marker.setIcon(icon);
            if (wasHidden) syncRetainedMarkerAccessibility(marker, false);
        }
        this.renderedIcons.set(marker, { ...icon, anchor: icon.anchor ? { ...icon.anchor } : icon.anchor });
    }

    /**
     * 마커 반환 (풀로 돌려보내기)
     * 
     * @param id 마커 ID
     */
    public release(id: string): void {
        const marker = this.active.get(id);
        if (!marker) return;

        this.active.delete(id);
        // Detached/evicted markers must not retain a render's restaurant/state
        // closure or dispatch a queued click for a no-longer-active place.
        marker.__onClick = undefined;
        syncRetainedMarkerAccessibility(marker, true);
        const detached = detachNaverMapMarker(marker);
        if (detached && this.pool.length < this.MAX_POOL_SIZE) {
            this.pool.push(marker);
        } else {
            marker.__disposeClickListener?.();
            marker.__disposeClickListener = undefined;
        }
        this.stats.released++;
    }

    /**
     * 여러 마커 한번에 반환
     * 
     * @param ids 마커 ID 배열
     */
    public releaseMultiple(ids: string[]): void {
        // The pool is a stack. Reverse a batch's release order so a returning
        // render in the same ID order reacquires its own icon/position rather
        // than rebuilding every SDK icon for a different restaurant.
        for (let index = ids.length - 1; index >= 0; index--) {
            this.release(ids[index]);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 모든 마커 반환
     */
    public releaseAll(): void {
        const ids = Array.from(this.active.keys());
        this.releaseMultiple(ids);
    }

    /**
     * 특정 ID 목록만 유지하고 나머지 반환
     * 
     * @param keepIds 유지할 마커 ID 배열
     */
    public releaseExcept(keepIds: Set<string>): void {
        const toRelease = Array.from(this.active.keys()).filter(
            (id) => !keepIds.has(id)
        );
        this.releaseMultiple(toRelease);
    }

    /**
     * 마커 업데이트 (위치나 아이콘 변경)
     * 
     * @param id 마커 ID
     * @param updates 업데이트할 속성
     */
    public update(
        id: string,
        updates: {
            position?: unknown;
            icon?: MarkerIconLike;
            zIndex?: number;
        }
    ): void {
        const marker = this.active.get(id);
        if (!marker) return;

        if (updates.position) {
            marker.setPosition(updates.position);
        }
        if (updates.icon) {
            this.applyIcon(marker, updates.icon);
        }
        if (updates.zIndex !== undefined) {
            marker.setZIndex(updates.zIndex);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 활성 마커 가져오기
     * 
     * @param id 마커 ID
     * @returns 마커 객체 (없으면 undefined)
     */
    public get(id: string): PooledMarker | undefined {
        return this.active.get(id);
    }

    /**
     * 마커 존재 여부 확인
     * 
     * @param id 마커 ID
     * @returns 존재하면 true
     */
    public has(id: string): boolean {
        return this.active.has(id);
    }

    /**
     * 풀 전체 정리 (컴포넌트 언마운트 시)
     */
    public clear(): void {
        this.active.forEach((marker) => {
            marker.__onClick = undefined;
            syncRetainedMarkerAccessibility(marker, true);
            detachNaverMapMarker(marker);
            marker.__disposeClickListener?.();
            marker.__disposeClickListener = undefined;
        });

        // 풀의 모든 마커도 정리
        this.pool.forEach((marker) => {
            marker.__onClick = undefined;
            detachNaverMapMarker(marker);
            marker.__disposeClickListener?.();
            marker.__disposeClickListener = undefined;
        });

        this.active.clear();
        this.pool = [];
        this.renderedIcons = new WeakMap();
    }

    /**
     * 통계 정보 가져오기 (디버깅용)
     */
    public getStats() {
        return {
            ...this.stats,
            activeCount: this.active.size,
            poolSize: this.pool.length,
            hitRate: this.stats.reused / (this.stats.created + this.stats.reused) || 0,
        };
    }

    /**
     * 통계 초기화
     */
    public resetStats(): void {
        this.stats = {
            created: 0,
            reused: 0,
            released: 0,
        };
    }

    /**
     * 콘솔에 통계 출력
     */
    public logStats(): void {
        const stats = this.getStats();
        console.table({
            'Active Markers': stats.activeCount,
            'Pool Size': stats.poolSize,
            'Total Created': stats.created,
            'Total Reused': stats.reused,
            'Total Released': stats.released,
            'Hit Rate': `${(stats.hitRate * 100).toFixed(1)}%`,
        });
    }
}

/**
 * 마커 풀 싱글톤 인스턴스 (편의 export)
 */
export const markerPool = MarkerPool.getInstance();

===== BEGIN FILE apps/web/lib/retained-marker-accessibility.ts bytes=3655 sha256=bf15c5fb1681e294c0be5b65a17e007570e2ebfb16af0868b41e96f5a2ed9ae4 =====
interface RetainedMarkerAccessibility {
    getElement: () => HTMLElement | null;
    __tzudongRetainedMarkerAriaHidden?: boolean;
}

const OWNER_ATTRIBUTE = 'data-tzudong-retained-marker-hidden';
const MARKER_SELECTOR = '[data-testid="marker"]';
const OVERLAY_SELECTOR = '[data-visible-marker-review-bubble-anchor="true"]';

interface PendingRootRegistry {
    target: HTMLElement;
    markers: Set<RetainedMarkerAccessibility>;
    observer: MutationObserver;
}

const rootTargets = new WeakMap<RetainedMarkerAccessibility, HTMLElement>();
const pendingRoots = new WeakMap<RetainedMarkerAccessibility, PendingRootRegistry>();
const rootRegistries = new WeakMap<HTMLElement, PendingRootRegistry>();

function cancelPendingRoot(marker: RetainedMarkerAccessibility): void {
    const registry = pendingRoots.get(marker);
    if (!registry) return;
    pendingRoots.delete(marker);
    registry.markers.delete(marker);
    if (registry.markers.size === 0) {
        registry.observer.disconnect();
        rootRegistries.delete(registry.target);
    }
}

function watchPendingRoot(marker: RetainedMarkerAccessibility, target: HTMLElement): void {
    const previous = pendingRoots.get(marker);
    if (previous?.target === target) return;
    cancelPendingRoot(marker);
    let registry = rootRegistries.get(target);
    if (!registry) {
        const markers = new Set<RetainedMarkerAccessibility>();
        const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
            // One observer per map, with no timer/poll loop. SDK insertion runs
            // this before paint; release/visibility/unmount cancel ownership.
            for (const pending of markers) syncRetainedMarkerAccessibility(pending, false, target);
        });
        registry = { target, markers, observer };
        rootRegistries.set(target, registry);
        observer.observe(target, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }
    registry.markers.add(marker);
    pendingRoots.set(marker, registry);
}

/** Keep retained SDK objects out of the mobile AX tree beyond the padded view. */
export function syncRetainedMarkerAccessibility(
    marker: RetainedMarkerAccessibility,
    visible: boolean,
    rootReadyTarget?: HTMLElement | null,
): void {
    const hidden = !visible;
    if (visible) {
        cancelPendingRoot(marker);
        rootTargets.delete(marker);
    } else if (rootReadyTarget) {
        rootTargets.set(marker, rootReadyTarget);
    }
    if (!hidden && !marker.__tzudongRetainedMarkerAriaHidden) return;

    const element = marker.getElement();
    const markerRoot = element?.matches?.(MARKER_SELECTOR)
        ? element
        : element?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(MARKER_SELECTOR);
    // The review bubble is a sibling of the marker inside this app-owned
    // anchor, so hide the complete overlay rather than only its image node.
    const root = markerRoot?.closest<HTMLElement>(OVERLAY_SELECTOR) ?? markerRoot;
    if (!root) {
        marker.__tzudongRetainedMarkerAriaHidden = hidden;
        const target = rootTargets.get(marker);
        if (hidden && target) watchPendingRoot(marker, target);
        return;
    }

    cancelPendingRoot(marker);

    if (hidden) {
        // Do not claim or later remove an aria-hidden state owned elsewhere.
        if (root.getAttribute('aria-hidden') !== 'true') {
            root.setAttribute(OWNER_ATTRIBUTE, 'true');
            root.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
        }
    } else if (root.getAttribute(OWNER_ATTRIBUTE) === 'true') {
        root.removeAttribute(OWNER_ATTRIBUTE);
        root.removeAttribute('aria-hidden');
    }
    marker.__tzudongRetainedMarkerAriaHidden = hidden;
}

===== BEGIN FILE apps/web/lib/naver-map-event-cleanup.ts bytes=1747 sha256=ee4c8bef6103c98b75238cc45eb2f119b1f5eab01202fb44979b05007c3957d6 =====
/** Provider teardown can fail after an SDK/auth failure; local cleanup must still finish. */
export function removeNaverMapListener<T>(
    events: { removeListener: (listener: T) => unknown } | null | undefined,
    listener: T | null | undefined,
): boolean {
    if (!events || listener == null) return false;
    try {
        events.removeListener(listener);
        return true;
    } catch {
        // Teardown only: do not let an invalid provider registry abort other cleanup.
        return false;
    }
}

/** A provider may retain an idle handler even when removal fails. */
export function createNaverOneShotCallback(callback: () => void) {
    let active = true;
    return {
        handle() {
            if (!active) return;
            active = false;
            callback();
        },
        cancel() { active = false; },
    };
}

export function createNaverCancellableCallback(callback: () => void) {
    let active = true;
    return {
        handle() { if (active) callback(); },
        cancel() { active = false; },
    };
}

/** Failed SDK instances must be discarded rather than returned to the marker pool. */
export function detachNaverMapMarker(marker: { setMap: (map: null) => void } | null | undefined): boolean {
    if (!marker) return false;
    try {
        marker.setMap(null);
        return true;
    } catch {
        return false;
    }
}

/** Remove the app-owned location visual immediately; retain failed SDK handles for retry. */
export function clearNaverDeviceLocationMarker<T extends { setMap: (map: null) => void }>(
    marker: T | null,
    removeVisual: () => void,
): T | null {
    const detached = detachNaverMapMarker(marker);
    removeVisual();
    return detached ? null : marker;
}

const YOUTUBE_VIDEO_ID_PATTERN = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{11}$/;
const MAX_YOUTUBE_URL_LENGTH = 2_048;

const WATCH_QUERY_PARAMETERS = new Set(['v', 't', 'start', 'end', 'feature', 'si']);
const SHORT_QUERY_PARAMETERS = new Set(['t', 'start', 'end', 'feature', 'si']);

function isCanonicalVideoId(value: string): boolean {
  return YOUTUBE_VIDEO_ID_PATTERN.test(value);
}

function isSafeInput(value: string): boolean {
  return (
    value.length > 0 &&
    value.length <= MAX_YOUTUBE_URL_LENGTH &&
    /^[\x21-\x7E]+$/.test(value) &&
    !value.includes('%') &&
    !value.includes('\\')
  );
}

function hasAllowedQueryParameters(
  search: string,
  allowedParameters: ReadonlySet<string>,
  requiredVideoId: string | null,
): boolean {
  if (!search) return requiredVideoId === null;

  const seenParameters = new Set<string>();
  const queryParts = search.slice(1).split('&');

  for (const queryPart of queryParts) {
    const separatorIndex = queryPart.indexOf('=');
    if (separatorIndex <= 0 || separatorIndex !== queryPart.lastIndexOf('=')) return false;

    const key = queryPart.slice(0, separatorIndex);
    const value = queryPart.slice(separatorIndex + 1);

    if (!allowedParameters.has(key) || seenParameters.has(key) || !value) return false;
    seenParameters.add(key);

    if (key === 'v') {
      if (!isCanonicalVideoId(value)) return false;
      continue;
    }

    if (key === 't' && !/^[0-9hms]+$/i.test(value)) return false;
    if ((key === 'start' || key === 'end') && !/^\d{1,10}$/.test(value)) return false;
    if ((key === 'feature' || key === 'si') && !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}$/.test(value)) return false;
  }

  return requiredVideoId === null || (seenParameters.has('v') && queryParts.some((part) => part === `v=${requiredVideoId}`));
}

function extractVideoIdFromCanonicalUrl(value: string): string | null {
  let url: URL;

  try {
    url = new URL(value);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }

  if (url.protocol !== 'https:' || url.username || url.password || url.port || url.hash) return null;

  const rawUrl = /^https:\/\/([^/?#]*)(\/[^?#]*)?(?:\?[^#]*)?$/i.exec(value);
  const authority = rawUrl?.[1]?.toLowerCase();
  const rawPath = rawUrl?.[2] ?? '';
  if (authority !== 'www.youtube.com' && authority !== 'youtu.be') return null;

  if (url.hostname === 'www.youtube.com') {
    if (rawPath !== '/watch' || url.pathname !== '/watch') return null;

    const videoId = url.searchParams.get('v');
    if (!videoId || !isCanonicalVideoId(videoId)) return null;
    if (!hasAllowedQueryParameters(url.search, WATCH_QUERY_PARAMETERS, videoId)) return null;

    return videoId;
  }

  if (url.hostname !== 'youtu.be') return null;

  const videoId = url.pathname.slice(1);
  if (rawPath !== `/${videoId}` || !isCanonicalVideoId(videoId) || url.pathname !== `/${videoId}`) return null;
  if (!hasAllowedQueryParameters(url.search, SHORT_QUERY_PARAMETERS, null)) return null;

  return videoId;
}

export function extractCanonicalYouTubeVideoId(value: unknown): string | null {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || /[\u0000-\u001F\u007F-\u009F]/.test(value)) return null;

  const candidate = value.trim();
  if (!isSafeInput(candidate)) return null;
  if (isCanonicalVideoId(candidate)) return candidate;

  return extractVideoIdFromCanonicalUrl(candidate);
}

export function buildCanonicalYouTubeWatchUrl(videoId: unknown): string | null {
  if (typeof videoId !== 'string' || !isCanonicalVideoId(videoId)) return null;

  return `https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=${videoId}`;
}

export function normalizeCanonicalYouTubeWatchUrl(value: unknown): string | null {
  return buildCanonicalYouTubeWatchUrl(extractCanonicalYouTubeVideoId(value));
}

const FLAME_SVG = '<svg class="tz-popularity-flame" viewBox="0 0 32 42" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false"><path class="tz-popularity-flame-outer" stroke="#fff7ed" stroke-width="1.4" d="M17 1c3 12-7 14-6 22 3-2 5-5 5-8 12 9 17 26 0 26C-3 41-3 24 6 15c0 6 1 8 3 9C6 15 19 12 17 1Z"/><path class="tz-popularity-flame-core" d="M17 24c1 6-5 7-4 11 5 4 12-2 4-11Z"/></svg>';

const POPULARITY_TIER_RANK = { standard: 0, rainbow: 1, ember: 2, fire: 3 } as const;

export function publicPopularityTier(value: unknown): "fire" | "ember" | "rainbow" | "standard" {
  return value === "fire" || value === "ember" || value === "rainbow" ? value : "standard";
}

export function bestPopularityTier(current: unknown, next: unknown): "fire" | "ember" | "rainbow" | "standard" {
  const left = publicPopularityTier(current);
  const right = publicPopularityTier(next);
  return POPULARITY_TIER_RANK[right] > POPULARITY_TIER_RANK[left] ? right : left;
}

export function popularityFlameMarkup(tier: unknown): string {
  const safeTier = publicPopularityTier(tier);
  if (safeTier === "standard") return "";
  return `<style>${POPULARITY_MARKER_CSS}</style>${FLAME_SVG}`;
}

export function wrapMarkerWithPopularityTier(innerHtml: string, tier: "fire" | "ember" | "rainbow" | "standard" | null | undefined): string {
  const safeTier = publicPopularityTier(tier);
  return `<style>${POPULARITY_MARKER_CSS}</style><div class="tz-popularity-marker" data-popularity-tier="${safeTier}">${innerHtml}${safeTier === "standard" ? "" : FLAME_SVG}</div>`;
}

export function syncPopularityMarkerInteraction(
  element: { setAttribute(name: string, value: string): void; removeAttribute(name: string): void },
  interacting: boolean,
): void {
  if (interacting) element.setAttribute("data-map-interacting", "true");
  else element.removeAttribute("data-map-interacting");
}

===== BEGIN FILE apps/web/lib/evidence-engineering/scoring.ts bytes=7062 sha256=0e3f195bf06636d6a8718378ac7a93eccbd3f439ee5d6ab5eebedbbaa6d02276 =====
import { THESIS_HYPERBOLIC_LAMBDA } from "./constants";
import { EvidenceContractCodes, EvidenceContractError } from "./errors";
import { requireYouTubeVideoId, safeCount } from "./guards";
import type { PopularitySignals, UniqueCountAggregate } from "./types";

export function decay(days: number, floor: number, halfChangeDays: number): number {
  if (![days, floor, halfChangeDays].every(Number.isFinite)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.nonFinite);
  }
  if (days < 0 || floor < 0 || floor >= 1 || halfChangeDays <= 0) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidDecayParameters);
  }
  return floor + (1 - floor) / (1 + days / halfChangeDays);
}

export function hyperbolicDecay(days: number, lambda: number = THESIS_HYPERBOLIC_LAMBDA): number {
  if (!Number.isFinite(days) || !Number.isFinite(lambda)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.nonFinite);
  }
  if (days < 0 || lambda <= 0) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidDecayParameters);
  }
  return 1 / (1 + lambda * days);
}

export function hyperbolicHalfLifeDays(lambda: number): number {
  if (!Number.isFinite(lambda)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.nonFinite);
  }
  if (lambda <= 0) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidDecayParameters);
  }
  const halfLife = 1 / lambda;
  if (!Number.isFinite(halfLife)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.nonFinite);
  }
  return halfLife;
}

export function thesisHalfLifeClaimIsExact(): boolean {
  return hyperbolicHalfLifeDays(THESIS_HYPERBOLIC_LAMBDA) === 460;
}

export function visitsMultiplier(videoIds: readonly string[], gamma: number): number {
  if (!Number.isFinite(gamma) || gamma < 0) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidGamma);
  }
  const uniqueIds = new Set<string>();
  for (const videoId of videoIds) {
    uniqueIds.add(requireYouTubeVideoId(videoId));
  }
  const multiplier = 1 + gamma * Math.log1p(Math.max(0, uniqueIds.size - 1));
  if (!Number.isFinite(multiplier)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.nonFinite);
  }
  return multiplier;
}

export function platformReviewSignal(reviewCount: unknown, maximum: number): number | null {
  return safeCount(reviewCount, maximum);
}

export function assemblePopularitySignals(input: {
  videoEngagement: number;
  daysSincePublish: number;
  floor: number;
  halfChangeDays: number;
  uniqueVideoIds: readonly string[];
  gamma: number;
  platformReviewSignal: number | null;
}): PopularitySignals {
  if (!Number.isFinite(input.videoEngagement) || input.videoEngagement < 0) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.nonFinite);
  }
  if (
    input.platformReviewSignal !== null &&
    (!Number.isFinite(input.platformReviewSignal) || input.platformReviewSignal < 0)
  ) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.nonFinite);
  }
  return {
    video_engagement: input.videoEngagement,
    freshness: decay(input.daysSincePublish, input.floor, input.halfChangeDays),
    visit_multiplier: visitsMultiplier(input.uniqueVideoIds, input.gamma),
    platform_review_signal: input.platformReviewSignal,
  };
}

export function aggregateUniqueCounts(
  rows: readonly { videoId: string; count: unknown }[],
  maximum: number,
): UniqueCountAggregate {
  const grouped = new Map<string, { count: number | null; seen: boolean }>();
  const order: string[] = [];

  for (const row of rows) {
    const videoId = requireYouTubeVideoId(row.videoId);
    const parsed = safeCount(row.count, maximum);
    const existing = grouped.get(videoId);
    if (!existing) {
      grouped.set(videoId, { count: parsed, seen: true });
      order.push(videoId);
      continue;
    }
    if (existing.count !== parsed) {
      throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.duplicateVideoConflict);
    }
  }

  let knownSum = 0;
  const missingVideoIds: string[] = [];
  for (const videoId of order) {
    const groupedCount = grouped.get(videoId);
    if (!groupedCount) continue;
    if (groupedCount.count === null) {
      missingVideoIds.push(videoId);
      continue;
    }
    knownSum += groupedCount.count;
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(knownSum)) {
      throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.nonFinite);
    }
  }

  return {
    videoCount: order.length,
    knownSum,
    missingVideoIds,
  };
}

export type CohortShadowMember = {
  identity: string;
  operational_score: number | null;
  candidate_signals: { freshness: number; visit_multiplier: number } | null;
};

export function compareCohortShadow(members: readonly CohortShadowMember[]): {
  records: Array<{
    identity: string;
    operational_score: number | null;
    candidate_freshness: number | null;
    candidate_visit_multiplier: number | null;
    comparison_present: boolean;
    differs: null;
  }>;
  summary: { count: number; differ_count: null; live_write_allowed: false };
} {
  if (members.length === 0) throw new Error("empty_cohort");
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const records = members.map((member) => {
    const identity = member.identity;
    if (typeof identity !== "string" || identity.trim() === "" || seen.has(identity)) {
      throw new Error("invalid_shadow_member");
    }
    seen.add(identity);
    const operational = member.operational_score;
    if (operational !== null && !Number.isFinite(operational)) {
      throw new Error("invalid_operational_score");
    }
    const signals = member.candidate_signals;
    if (signals === null) {
      return {
        identity,
        operational_score: operational,
        candidate_freshness: null,
        candidate_visit_multiplier: null,
        comparison_present: false,
        differs: null,
      };
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(signals.freshness) || !Number.isFinite(signals.visit_multiplier)) {
      throw new Error("invalid_candidate_signals");
    }
    // These two dimensionless signals have no approved scalar score mapping.
    // Their presence cannot establish a difference from an operational score.
    return {
      identity,
      operational_score: operational,
      candidate_freshness: signals.freshness,
      candidate_visit_multiplier: signals.visit_multiplier,
      comparison_present: false,
      differs: null,
    };
  });
  return {
    records,
    summary: {
      count: records.length,
      differ_count: null,
      live_write_allowed: false,
    },
  };
}

export function refuseOperationalWrite(summary: { live_write_allowed: boolean }): {
  popularity_score: null;
  popularity_tier: null;
  writes_hosted_database: false;
  mutates_restaurant_updated_at: false;
  overwrites_operational_values: false;
} {
  if (summary.live_write_allowed !== true) throw new Error("shadow_comparison_required");
  return {
    popularity_score: null,
    popularity_tier: null,
    writes_hosted_database: false,
    mutates_restaurant_updated_at: false,
    overwrites_operational_values: false,
  };
}

===== BEGIN FILE apps/web/lib/evidence-engineering/frames.ts bytes=9618 sha256=c2b9c3fb2fca0f6fa229aeb0c62a222f22b11441cefc40d35248e0f6ca6f3dc8 =====
import { buildCanonicalYouTubeWatchUrl } from "../youtube-url";
import {
  FRAME_SAMPLING,
  LEVEL1_FRAME_TYPES,
} from "./constants";
import { formatStorageObjectKey } from "./storage-key.mjs";
import { EvidenceContractCodes, EvidenceContractError } from "./errors";
import {
  assertNoForbiddenPayloadKeys,
  isConfidenceLevel,
  isEvidenceFrameType,
  isEvidenceLevel,
  isModerationStatus,
  parseMember,
  requireContentSha256,
  requireDetectedName,
  requireFrameId,
  requireImageUrl,
  requireTimestampMs,
  requireYouTubeVideoId,
} from "./guards";
import type {
  EvidenceFrameDraft,
  FrameSamplingPlan,
  RestaurantEvidenceFrame,
} from "./types";

const FRAME_PAYLOAD_KEYS = [
  "frame_id",
  "content_sha256",
  "video_id",
  "timestamp_ms",
  "storage_object_key",
  "youtube_seek_url",
  "frame_type",
  "evidence_level",
  "confidence_level",
  "moderation_status",
  "detected_shop_name",
  "detected_branch_name",
  "image_url",
] as const;

export function buildEvidenceSeekUrl(videoId: string, timestampMs: number): string {
  const id = requireYouTubeVideoId(videoId);
  const timestamp = requireTimestampMs(timestampMs);
  const watchUrl = buildCanonicalYouTubeWatchUrl(id);
  if (!watchUrl) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidVideoId);
  }
  return `${watchUrl}&t=${Math.floor(timestamp / 1000)}`;
}

export function buildStorageObjectKey(videoId: string, timestampMs: number, contentSha256: string): string {
  const id = requireYouTubeVideoId(videoId);
  const timestamp = requireTimestampMs(timestampMs);
  const hash = requireContentSha256(contentSha256);
  return formatStorageObjectKey(id, timestamp, hash);
}

export function planFrameSamplingWindow(videoId: string, targetTimestampMs: number): FrameSamplingPlan {
  const id = requireYouTubeVideoId(videoId);
  const target = requireTimestampMs(targetTimestampMs);
  const end = requireTimestampMs(target + FRAME_SAMPLING.windowMs);
  return {
    video_id: id,
    target_timestamp_ms: target,
    window_start_ms: Math.max(0, target - FRAME_SAMPLING.windowMs),
    window_end_ms: end,
    sample_fps: FRAME_SAMPLING.sampleFpsAfterDecode,
    keyframe_role: FRAME_SAMPLING.keyframeRole,
    loads_full_video_in_node: false,
  };
}

export function isLevel1SourceObserved(
  frame: Pick<RestaurantEvidenceFrame, "evidence_level" | "frame_type" | "moderation_status">,
): boolean {
  return (
    frame.moderation_status === "approved" &&
    frame.evidence_level === "SOURCE_OBSERVED" &&
    (LEVEL1_FRAME_TYPES as readonly string[]).includes(frame.frame_type)
  );
}

export function buildEvidenceFrame(draft: EvidenceFrameDraft): RestaurantEvidenceFrame {
  const videoId = requireYouTubeVideoId(draft.video_id);
  const timestampMs = requireTimestampMs(draft.timestamp_ms);
  const contentSha256 = requireContentSha256(draft.content_sha256);
  const frame: RestaurantEvidenceFrame = {
    frame_id: requireFrameId(draft.frame_id),
    content_sha256: contentSha256,
    video_id: videoId,
    timestamp_ms: timestampMs,
    storage_object_key: buildStorageObjectKey(videoId, timestampMs, contentSha256),
    youtube_seek_url: buildEvidenceSeekUrl(videoId, timestampMs),
    frame_type: parseMember(draft.frame_type, isEvidenceFrameType, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidFrameType),
    evidence_level: parseMember(draft.evidence_level, isEvidenceLevel, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidEvidenceLevel),
    confidence_level: parseMember(draft.confidence_level, isConfidenceLevel, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidConfidence),
    moderation_status: parseMember(draft.moderation_status, isModerationStatus, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidModeration),
    detected_shop_name: requireDetectedName(draft.detected_shop_name),
    detected_branch_name: requireDetectedName(draft.detected_branch_name),
    image_url: requireImageUrl(draft.image_url),
  };
  return frame;
}

export function parseEvidenceFrame(input: unknown): RestaurantEvidenceFrame {
  if (input === null || typeof input !== "object" || Array.isArray(input)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidFrame);
  }
  assertNoForbiddenPayloadKeys(input);
  const record = input as Record<string, unknown>;
  for (const key of Object.keys(record)) {
    if (!(FRAME_PAYLOAD_KEYS as readonly string[]).includes(key)) {
      throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidFrame);
    }
  }
  const frame = buildEvidenceFrame({
    frame_id: readString(record.frame_id, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidFrameId),
    content_sha256: readString(record.content_sha256, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidContentSha256),
    video_id: readString(record.video_id, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidVideoId),
    timestamp_ms: readTimestamp(record.timestamp_ms),
    frame_type: record.frame_type as EvidenceFrameDraft["frame_type"],
    evidence_level: record.evidence_level as EvidenceFrameDraft["evidence_level"],
    confidence_level: record.confidence_level as EvidenceFrameDraft["confidence_level"],
    moderation_status: record.moderation_status as EvidenceFrameDraft["moderation_status"],
    detected_shop_name: readNullableString(record.detected_shop_name, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidDetectedName),
    detected_branch_name: readNullableString(record.detected_branch_name, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidDetectedName),
    image_url: readNullableString(record.image_url, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidImageUrl),
  });
  if (typeof record.storage_object_key === "string" && record.storage_object_key !== frame.storage_object_key) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.storageKeyMismatch);
  }
  if (typeof record.youtube_seek_url === "string" && record.youtube_seek_url !== frame.youtube_seek_url) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.seekIntegrityMismatch);
  }
  if ("storage_object_key" in record && typeof record.storage_object_key !== "string") {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.storageKeyMismatch);
  }
  if ("youtube_seek_url" in record && typeof record.youtube_seek_url !== "string") {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.seekIntegrityMismatch);
  }
  return frame;
}

function readString(value: unknown, code: EvidenceContractError["code"]): string {
  if (typeof value !== "string") throw new EvidenceContractError(code);
  return value;
}

function readNullableString(value: unknown, code: EvidenceContractError["code"]): string | null {
  if (value === null) return null;
  if (typeof value !== "string") throw new EvidenceContractError(code);
  return value;
}

function readTimestamp(value: unknown): number {
  if (typeof value !== "number") {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidTimestampMs);
  }
  return value;
}

function sameIdentity(left: RestaurantEvidenceFrame, right: RestaurantEvidenceFrame): boolean {
  return (
    left.content_sha256 === right.content_sha256 &&
    left.video_id === right.video_id &&
    left.timestamp_ms === right.timestamp_ms &&
    left.storage_object_key === right.storage_object_key &&
    left.youtube_seek_url === right.youtube_seek_url &&
    left.frame_type === right.frame_type &&
    left.evidence_level === right.evidence_level &&
    left.confidence_level === right.confidence_level &&
    left.detected_shop_name === right.detected_shop_name &&
    left.detected_branch_name === right.detected_branch_name
  );
}

// Public detail rendering must fail closed without taking down the restaurant panel.
export function approvedEvidenceFramesOrEmpty(groups: readonly RestaurantEvidenceFrame[][]): RestaurantEvidenceFrame[] {
  try {
    return unionApprovedEvidenceFrames(groups);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof EvidenceContractError) return [];
    throw error;
  }
}

export function unionApprovedEvidenceFrames(groups: readonly RestaurantEvidenceFrame[][]): RestaurantEvidenceFrame[] {
  const grouped = new Map<string, RestaurantEvidenceFrame[]>();
  for (const group of groups) {
    for (const frame of group) {
      const validated = parseEvidenceFrame(frame);
      const existing = grouped.get(validated.frame_id);
      if (existing) existing.push(validated);
      else grouped.set(validated.frame_id, [validated]);
    }
  }

  const approved: RestaurantEvidenceFrame[] = [];
  for (const copies of grouped.values()) {
    const anchor = copies[0];
    if (copies.some((copy) => !sameIdentity(anchor, copy))) {
      throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.duplicateFrameConflict);
    }
    const hasApproved = copies.some((copy) => copy.moderation_status === "approved");
    const hasRejected = copies.some((copy) => copy.moderation_status === "rejected");
    if (hasApproved && hasRejected) {
      throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.moderationConflict);
    }
    if (!hasApproved) continue;
    const approvedCopies = copies.filter((copy) => copy.moderation_status === "approved");
    const imageUrls = new Set(approvedCopies.map((copy) => copy.image_url));
    const imageUrl = imageUrls.size === 1 ? approvedCopies[0].image_url : null;
    approved.push(buildEvidenceFrame({ ...approvedCopies[0], image_url: imageUrl, moderation_status: "approved" }));
  }

  approved.sort((left, right) => (
    left.timestamp_ms - right.timestamp_ms || left.frame_id.localeCompare(right.frame_id)
  ));
  const storageKeys = new Set<string>();
  for (const frame of approved) {
    if (storageKeys.has(frame.storage_object_key)) {
      throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.duplicateFrameConflict);
    }
    storageKeys.add(frame.storage_object_key);
  }
  return approved;
}

import { extractCanonicalYouTubeVideoId } from "../youtube-url";
import {
  CALENDAR_DATE_PATTERN,
  CANONICAL_KEY_PATTERN,
  CONFIDENCE_LEVELS,
  CONTENT_SHA256_PATTERN,
  DETECTED_NAME_MAX_LENGTH,
  EVIDENCE_FRAME_TYPES,
  EVIDENCE_LEVELS,
  FORBIDDEN_PAYLOAD_KEYS,
  FRAME_ID_PATTERN,
  ISO_TIMESTAMP_PATTERN,
  MODERATION_STATUSES,
  POPULARITY_TIERS,
  VERSION_TOKEN_PATTERN,
} from "./constants";
import { EvidenceContractCodes, EvidenceContractError } from "./errors";
import type {
  ConfidenceLevel,
  EvidenceFrameType,
  EvidenceLevel,
  ModerationStatus,
  PopularityTier,
} from "./types";

const PHONE_LIKE_PATTERN = /\d{2,4}-\d{3,4}-\d{4}/;

export function requireYouTubeVideoId(value: string): string {
  const videoId = extractCanonicalYouTubeVideoId(value);
  if (!videoId || videoId !== value) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidVideoId);
  }
  return videoId;
}

export function requireTimestampMs(value: number): number {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < 0) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidTimestampMs);
  }
  return value;
}

export function requireContentSha256(value: string): string {
  if (!CONTENT_SHA256_PATTERN.test(value)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidContentSha256);
  }
  return value;
}

export function requireFrameId(value: string): string {
  if (!FRAME_ID_PATTERN.test(value)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidFrameId);
  }
  return value;
}

export function requireVersionToken(value: string, code: "invalid_scoring_version" | "invalid_cohort_version"): string {
  if (!VERSION_TOKEN_PATTERN.test(value)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(code);
  }
  return value;
}

export function requireIsoTimestamp(value: string): string {
  if (!ISO_TIMESTAMP_PATTERN.test(value) || !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value))) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidComputedAt);
  }
  return value;
}

export function requireCalendarDate(value: string): string {
  if (!CALENDAR_DATE_PATTERN.test(value)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidCalendarDate);
  }
  const [yearText, monthText, dayText] = value.split("-");
  const year = Number(yearText);
  const month = Number(monthText);
  const day = Number(dayText);
  const utc = new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day));
  if (
    utc.getUTCFullYear() !== year ||
    utc.getUTCMonth() !== month - 1 ||
    utc.getUTCDate() !== day
  ) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidCalendarDate);
  }
  return value;
}

export function requireCanonicalKey(value: string): string {
  if (!CANONICAL_KEY_PATTERN.test(value)) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidCanonicalKey);
  }
  return value;
}

export function requireBoundedText(value: string, maxLength: number, code: "invalid_detected_name" | "invalid_closure_record"): string {
  const trimmed = value.trim();
  if (
    trimmed.length === 0 ||
    trimmed.length > maxLength ||
    /[\u0000-\u001F\u007F]/.test(trimmed) ||
    trimmed.includes("@") ||
    PHONE_LIKE_PATTERN.test(trimmed)
  ) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(code);
  }
  return trimmed;
}

export function requireDetectedName(value: string | null): string | null {
  if (value === null) return null;
  if (typeof value !== "string") {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidDetectedName);
  }
  if (value.trim() === "") return null;
  return requireBoundedText(value, DETECTED_NAME_MAX_LENGTH, EvidenceContractCodes.invalidDetectedName);
}

export function requireImageUrl(value: string | null): string | null {
  if (value === null) return null;
  if (typeof value !== "string" || value.length === 0 || value.length > 2048) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidImageUrl);
  }
  let url: URL;
  try {
    url = new URL(value);
  } catch {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidImageUrl);
  }
  if (url.protocol !== "https:" || url.username || url.password) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidImageUrl);
  }
  return value;
}

export function assertNoForbiddenPayloadKeys(value: object): void {
  for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
    if ((FORBIDDEN_PAYLOAD_KEYS as readonly string[]).includes(key)) {
      throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.personalDataForbidden);
    }
  }
}

export function isEvidenceLevel(value: unknown): value is EvidenceLevel {
  return typeof value === "string" && (EVIDENCE_LEVELS as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

export function isEvidenceFrameType(value: unknown): value is EvidenceFrameType {
  return typeof value === "string" && (EVIDENCE_FRAME_TYPES as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

export function isConfidenceLevel(value: unknown): value is ConfidenceLevel {
  return typeof value === "string" && (CONFIDENCE_LEVELS as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

export function isModerationStatus(value: unknown): value is ModerationStatus {
  return typeof value === "string" && (MODERATION_STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

export function isPopularityTier(value: unknown): value is PopularityTier {
  return typeof value === "string" && (POPULARITY_TIERS as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

export function parseMember<T extends string>(
  value: unknown,
  guard: (value: unknown) => value is T,
  code: EvidenceContractError["code"],
): T {
  if (!guard(value)) throw new EvidenceContractError(code);
  return value;
}

/**
 * Parses a count the way the synthesis `safe_count` does.
 * Null means missing. Zero means a real zero. Booleans are missing.
 * Integral JavaScript numbers are accepted. Decimal strings are missing.
 * Underscores follow Python `int` digit rules. Values above the maximum clamp.
 */
export function safeCount(value: unknown, maximum: number): number | null {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(maximum) || maximum < 0) {
    throw new EvidenceContractError(EvidenceContractCodes.invalidMaximum);
  }
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return null;
  if (typeof value === "boolean") return null;
  if (typeof value === "number") {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < 0) return null;
    return Math.min(value, maximum);
  }
  if (typeof value !== "string") return null;
  const parsed = parsePythonInteger(value);
  if (parsed === null || parsed < 0) return null;
  return Math.min(parsed, maximum);
}

function parsePythonInteger(value: string): number | null {
  const stripped = value.replaceAll(",", "").trim();
  if (!/^[+-]?\d(?:_?\d)*$/.test(stripped)) return null;
  const digits = stripped.replaceAll("_", "");
  const negative = digits.startsWith("-");
  const magnitude = negative ? digits.slice(1) : digits.replace(/^\+/, "");
  if (!/^\d+$/.test(magnitude)) return null;
  const numeric = Number(magnitude);
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(numeric)) return null;
  return negative ? -numeric : numeric;
}

===== BEGIN FILE apps/web/lib/evidence-engineering/published-popularity.ts bytes=5687 sha256=84ee9979ace9756456178449f6d831fe796cee0d68d66e155fc127aaef4163f1 =====
import publication from './public-popularity-publication.json';
import type { Restaurant } from '@/types/restaurant';

export type PublishedPopularityTier = 'fire' | 'ember' | 'rainbow' | 'standard';
export type PublishedPopularityLookup = (restaurantId: string) => PublishedPopularityTier;
export interface PublicPopularityPublication {
    readonly schemaVersion: 2;
    readonly publicationVersion: string;
    readonly sourceRevision: string | null;
    readonly entries: readonly Readonly<{ restaurantId: string; memberRestaurantIds: readonly string[]; tier: PublishedPopularityTier }>[];
}

const MAX_PUBLICATIONS = 5000;
const MAX_PUBLICATION_BYTES = 512 * 1024;
const fail = (): never => { throw new Error('PUBLIC_POPULARITY_PUBLICATION_INVALID'); };

function exactKeys(value: Record<string, unknown>, keys: readonly string[]): boolean {
    const actual = Object.keys(value);
    return actual.length === keys.length && keys.every((key) => Object.hasOwn(value, key));
}

// Shape validation is not an operator approval receipt. The source is deliberately
// empty until an operator-reviewed cohort is published through protected Git.
// Never populate it automatically from the private metrics or shadow tables.
export function parsePublicPopularityPublication(value: unknown): PublicPopularityPublication {
    if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return fail();
    const data = value as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (!exactKeys(data, ['schemaVersion', 'publicationVersion', 'sourceRevision', 'entries'])
        || data.schemaVersion !== 2
        || typeof data.publicationVersion !== 'string'
        || !/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,63}$/.test(data.publicationVersion)
        || (data.sourceRevision !== null && (typeof data.sourceRevision !== 'string' || !/^[a-f0-9]{40}$/.test(data.sourceRevision)))
        || !Array.isArray(data.entries) || data.entries.length > MAX_PUBLICATIONS) return fail();
    if (data.entries.length > 0 && (data.publicationVersion === 'unpublished' || data.sourceRevision === null)) return fail();
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    const entries = data.entries.map((entry: unknown) => {
        if (!entry || typeof entry !== 'object' || Array.isArray(entry)) return fail();
        const row = entry as Record<string, unknown>;
        if (!exactKeys(row, ['restaurantId', 'memberRestaurantIds', 'tier'])
            || typeof row.restaurantId !== 'string' || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}$/.test(row.restaurantId)
            || typeof row.tier !== 'string' || !['fire', 'ember', 'rainbow', 'standard'].includes(row.tier)
            || !Array.isArray(row.memberRestaurantIds) || !row.memberRestaurantIds.length
            || row.memberRestaurantIds.length > MAX_PUBLICATIONS) return fail();
        const members: string[] = [];
        for (const member of row.memberRestaurantIds) {
            if (typeof member !== 'string' || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}$/.test(member)
                || seen.has(member) || (members.length > 0 && members[members.length - 1] >= member)
                || seen.size >= MAX_PUBLICATIONS) return fail();
            members.push(member);
            seen.add(member);
        }
        // Explicit reviewed membership binds the grade, not whichever video row
        // the compact/full projection selects as its display representative.
        if (members[0] !== row.restaurantId) return fail();
        return Object.freeze({ restaurantId: row.restaurantId,
            memberRestaurantIds: Object.freeze(members), tier: row.tier as PublishedPopularityTier });
    });
    const result = { schemaVersion: 2 as const, publicationVersion: data.publicationVersion,
        sourceRevision: data.sourceRevision as string | null, entries: Object.freeze(entries) };
    if (new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(result)).byteLength > MAX_PUBLICATION_BYTES) return fail();
    return Object.freeze(result);
}

function lookupFor(publication: PublicPopularityPublication): PublishedPopularityLookup {
    const tiers = new Map<string, PublishedPopularityTier>();
    for (const entry of publication.entries) {
        for (const member of entry.memberRestaurantIds) tiers.set(member, entry.tier);
    }
    return (restaurantId) => tiers.get(restaurantId) ?? 'standard';
}

export function createPublishedPopularityLookup(value: unknown): PublishedPopularityLookup {
    return lookupFor(parsePublicPopularityPublication(value));
}

export const publicPopularityPublication = parsePublicPopularityPublication(publication);
export const lookupPublishedPopularityTier = lookupFor(publicPopularityPublication);

export function resolvePublishedPopularityTier(
    restaurant: { id: string; mergedRestaurants?: readonly { id: string }[] | null },
    lookup: PublishedPopularityLookup = lookupPublishedPopularityTier,
    members = restaurant.mergedRestaurants,
): PublishedPopularityTier {
    if (lookup === lookupPublishedPopularityTier && publicPopularityPublication.entries.length === 0) return 'standard';
    const tier = lookup(restaurant.id);
    // A new/unpublished member or conflicting publication invalidates the group
    // display. Never choose the highest grade or trust the display representative.
    for (const member of members ?? []) {
        if (lookup(member.id) !== tier) return 'standard';
    }
    return tier;
}

export function projectPublishedPopularityTier(
    restaurant: Restaurant,
    lookup: PublishedPopularityLookup = lookupPublishedPopularityTier,
): Restaurant {
    const tier = resolvePublishedPopularityTier(restaurant, lookup);
    return restaurant.popularity_tier === tier ? restaurant : { ...restaurant, popularity_tier: tier };
}

===== BEGIN FILE apps/web/scripts/build-public-popularity.ts bytes=5900 sha256=566113925e5c6a8598b28be3bf84ef53e7e525c4d727977475d8693082bb7eeb =====
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { mergeRestaurants } from '../hooks/use-restaurants';
import { extractCanonicalYouTubeVideoId } from '../lib/youtube-url';
import { parsePublicPopularityPublication } from '../lib/evidence-engineering/published-popularity';
import { safeCount } from '../lib/evidence-engineering/guards';
import { restaurantMatchesOverseasCountry } from '../lib/overseas-region-matching';
import { OVERSEAS_REGIONS } from '../constants/overseas-regions';
import type { Tables } from '../integrations/supabase/types';

export async function buildPublicPopularity(sourcePath: string, metricsPath: string, revision: string) {
  const sourceBytes = await readFile(sourcePath);
  const metricBytes = await readFile(metricsPath);
  const source = JSON.parse(sourceBytes.toString()) as { rows: Array<Record<string, unknown>> };
  const metrics = JSON.parse(metricBytes.toString()) as { rows: Array<{ video_id: string; view_count: number | string; fetched_at: string; bucket_started_at: string }> };
  const byVideo = new Map<string, typeof metrics.rows[number]>();
  for (const row of metrics.rows) {
    const videoId = extractCanonicalYouTubeVideoId(row.video_id);
    if (!videoId) throw new Error('popularity_metrics_video_invalid');
    const previous = byVideo.get(videoId);
    if (previous && (safeCount(previous.view_count, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER) !== safeCount(row.view_count, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER)
      || (previous.fetched_at || previous.bucket_started_at) !== (row.fetched_at || row.bucket_started_at))) {
      throw new Error('popularity_metrics_video_conflict');
    }
    byVideo.set(videoId, { ...row, video_id: videoId });
  }
  const rows = source.rows.map((r) => {
    if (r.status !== 'approved') throw new Error('popularity_source_not_approved');
    const validCoordinate = (value: unknown, limit: number) =>
      (typeof value === 'number' || (typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() !== ''))
      && Number.isFinite(Number(value)) && Math.abs(Number(value)) <= limit;
    // Validate before mergeRestaurants can replace missing coordinates with zero.
    if (!validCoordinate(r.lat, 90) || !validCoordinate(r.lng, 180) || (Number(r.lat) === 0 && Number(r.lng) === 0)) {
      throw new Error('popularity_source_coordinates_invalid');
    }
    const name = r.approved_name ?? r.name;
    if (typeof name !== 'string' || name.trim() === '') throw new Error('popularity_source_name_invalid');
    return { ...r, name, approved_name: name, lat: Number(r.lat), lng: Number(r.lng) };
  }) as unknown as Tables<'restaurants'>[];
  const countries = [...new Set(Object.values(OVERSEAS_REGIONS).map((r) => r.country))];
  const groups = mergeRestaurants(rows, () => 'standard').map((r) => {
    const members = r.mergedRestaurants?.length ? r.mergedRestaurants : [r];
    const ids = [...new Set(members.map((m) => m.id))].sort();
    const videos = [...new Set(members.map((m) => extractCanonicalYouTubeVideoId(m.youtube_link ?? '')).filter((v): v is string => !!v))];
    const observed = videos.flatMap((id) => {
      const metric = byVideo.get(id); const count = safeCount(metric?.view_count, Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER);
      return metric && count !== null && count > 0 ? [{ videoId: id, count, observedAt: metric.fetched_at || metric.bucket_started_at }] : [];
    });
    const overseas = countries.some((country) => restaurantMatchesOverseasCountry(r, country)) || Number(r.lat) < 33 || Number(r.lat) > 39 || Number(r.lng) < 124 || Number(r.lng) > 132;
    return { restaurantId: ids[0], memberRestaurantIds: ids, name: r.name, cohort: overseas ? 'overseas' : 'domestic', viewCount: observed.length ? Math.max(...observed.map((m) => m.count)) : null, observed };
  });
  const thresholds = ['domestic', 'overseas'].map((cohort) => {
    const counts = groups.filter((g) => g.cohort === cohort && g.viewCount !== null).map((g) => g.viewCount!).sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const percentile = (p: number) => counts.length ? counts[Math.max(0, Math.ceil(counts.length * p) - 1)] : null;
    return { cohort, knownGroups: counts.length, fireAt: percentile(0.98), emberAt: percentile(0.95), rainbowAt: percentile(0.85) };
  });
  const entries = groups.map((group) => {
    const cut = thresholds.find((c) => c.cohort === group.cohort)!;
    const tier = group.viewCount === null || cut.fireAt === null ? 'standard' : group.viewCount >= cut.fireAt ? 'fire' : group.viewCount >= cut.emberAt! ? 'ember' : group.viewCount >= cut.rainbowAt! ? 'rainbow' : 'standard';
    return { restaurantId: group.restaurantId, memberRestaurantIds: group.memberRestaurantIds, tier };
  }).sort((a, b) => a.restaurantId.localeCompare(b.restaurantId));
  const publication = parsePublicPopularityPublication({ schemaVersion: 2, publicationVersion: 'video-views-20261023-v2', sourceRevision: revision, entries });
  const sha = (b: Buffer) => createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex');
  return { publication, evidence: { policy: 'map-cohort-max-linked-video-views-nearest-rank-p98-p95-p85-v2', definition: 'Most-viewed linked video per merged restaurant, ranked separately within domestic and overseas approved restaurant cohorts. This measures video reach, not food quality.', sourceSha256: sha(sourceBytes), metricsSha256: sha(metricBytes), thresholds, groups } };
}

if (import.meta.main) {
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  const result = await buildPublicPopularity(args[0], args[1], args[2]);
  await writeFile(args[3], JSON.stringify(result.publication, null, 2) + '\n');
  await writeFile(args[4], JSON.stringify(result.evidence, null, 2) + '\n');
  console.log(JSON.stringify({ groups: result.publication.entries.length, tiers: result.publication.entries.reduce((a, e) => ({ ...a, [e.tier]: (a[e.tier] ?? 0) + 1 }), {} as Record<string, number>), thresholds: result.evidence.thresholds }));
}

export async function fetchRestaurantsRecoveringMissingEmbed<T>(
    query: Array<[string, string | number]>,
    fetchRows: (nextQuery: Array<[string, string | number]>) => Promise<T[]> = (nextQuery) => fetchSupabaseRows<T>("restaurants", nextQuery),
): Promise<T[]> {
    try {
        return await fetchRows(query);
    } catch (error) {
        const select = query.find(([key]) => key === "select")?.[1];
        if (!isMissingEvidenceRelationError(error) || typeof select !== "string" || !select.includes("restaurant_evidence_frames(")) {
            throw error;
        }
        return fetchRows(query.map(([key, value]) => (
            key === "select" ? ["select", selectWithoutEvidenceEmbed(select)] : [key, value]
        )));
    }
}

type NormalizedBounds = [number, number, number, number];

const mergePerfCounters = {
    similarityChecks: 0,
    mainSelectionComparisons: 0,
};

/**
 * 레벤슈타인 거리 계산 (문자열 유사도 측정용)
 * 두 문자열 사이의 편집 거리를 계산합니다.
 *
 * @param str1 기준 문자열
 * @param str2 비교 대상 문자열
 * @param maxDistance 허용되는 최대 거리 (초과하면 즉시 종료)
 * @returns 편집 거리 (숫자)
 */
function levenshteinDistance(str1: string, str2: string, maxDistance: number = Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER): number {
    const len1 = str1.length;
    const len2 = str2.length;

    if (Math.abs(len1 - len2) > maxDistance) return maxDistance + 1;
    if (len1 === 0) return Math.min(len2, maxDistance + 1);
    if (len2 === 0) return Math.min(len1, maxDistance + 1);

    const normalizedStr1 = len1 >= len2 ? str1 : str2;
    const normalizedStr2 = len1 >= len2 ? str2 : str1;
    const n = normalizedStr1.length;
    const m = normalizedStr2.length;

    let previous = new Int32Array(m + 1);
    let current = new Int32Array(m + 1);

    for (let j = 0; j <= m; j++) {
        previous[j] = j;
    }

    for (let i = 1; i <= n; i++) {
        current[0] = i;
        let rowMin = current[0];

        const windowStart = Math.max(1, i - maxDistance);
        const windowEnd = Math.min(m, i + maxDistance);

        for (let j = 1; j < windowStart; j++) {
            current[j] = maxDistance + 1;
        }

        for (let j = windowStart; j <= windowEnd; j++) {
            const deletion = previous[j] + 1;
            const insertion = current[j - 1] + 1;
            const substitution = previous[j - 1] + (normalizedStr1[i - 1] === normalizedStr2[j - 1] ? 0 : 1);
            const cost = Math.min(deletion, insertion, substitution);
            current[j] = cost;
            if (cost < rowMin) {
                rowMin = cost;
            }
        }

        for (let j = windowEnd + 1; j <= m; j++) {
            current[j] = maxDistance + 1;
        }

        if (rowMin > maxDistance) {
            return maxDistance + 1;
        }

        const temp = previous;
        previous = current;
        current = temp;
    }

    const distance = previous[m];
    return distance > maxDistance ? maxDistance + 1 : distance;
}

/**
 * 문자열 유사도 계산 함수
 * 0-1 사이의 값으로 반환하며, 1에 가까울수록 두 문자열이 유사합니다.
 *
 * @param str1 기준 문자열
 * @param str2 비교 대상 문자열
 * @param similarityThreshold similarity 임계값
 * @returns 유사도 (0.0 ~ 1.0)
 */
function calculateSimilarity(str1: string, str2: string, similarityThreshold = SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD): number {
    const maxLen = Math.max(str1.length, str2.length);
    if (maxLen === 0) return 1.0;

    mergePerfCounters.similarityChecks += 1;

    const maxDistance = Math.floor(maxLen * (1 - similarityThreshold));
    if (maxDistance <= 0) {
        return str1 === str2 ? 1.0 : 0;
    }

    const distance = levenshteinDistance(str1, str2, maxDistance);
    if (distance > maxDistance) return 0;

    return 1 - distance / maxLen;
}

/**
 * 주소 정규화 함수
 * 층/호수 정보를 제거하고, 공백과 특수문자를 제거하여 비교 용이성을 높입니다.
 *
 * @param address 원본 주소 문자열
 * @returns 정규화된 주소 문자열
 */
function normalizeAddress(address: string): string {
    return address
        // 층/호수 정보 제거 (같은 건물 다른 층은 같은 주소로 취급)
        .replace(/지하\s*\d+\s*층/g, '')
        .replace(/지상\s*\d+\s*층/g, '')
        .replace(/\d+\s*층/g, '')
        .replace(/\d+\s*호/g, '')
        // 공백 및 특수문자 제거
        .replace(/\s+/g, '')
        .replace(/[^\w가-힣]/g, '')
        .toLowerCase();
}

function getRestaurantName(restaurant: RestaurantWithOptionalName): string {
    return restaurant.name || restaurant.approved_name || '';
}

function isLengthDiffWithinSimilarityThreshold(
    str1: string,
    str2: string,
    threshold = SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD
): boolean {
    const maxLen = Math.max(str1.length, str2.length);
    if (maxLen === 0) return true;
    const lenDiff = Math.abs(str1.length - str2.length);
    return lenDiff <= maxLen * (1 - threshold);
}

function normalizeBounds(bounds?: UseRestaurantsOptions["bounds"]): NormalizedBounds | null {
    if (!bounds) return null;
    const round = (value: number) => Number(value.toFixed(QUERY_KEY_COORDINATE_PRECISION));
    return [round(bounds.south), round(bounds.west), round(bounds.north), round(bounds.east)];
}

function normalizeCategories(category?: string[]): string[] {
    if (!category || category.length === 0) return [];
    const normalized = category
        .map((item) => item?.trim())
        .filter((item): item is string => Boolean(item));

    const deduped = new Set(normalized);
    return [...deduped].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));
}

function normalizeRegion(region?: Region): string | null {
    const normalized = region?.trim();
    return normalized ? normalized : null;
}

    return typeof minReviews === "number" && Number.isFinite(minReviews) && minReviews > 0 ? minReviews : null;
}

}
