===== BEGIN FILE apps/web/tests-unit/visible-marker-review-bubbles.test.ts bytes=3456 sha256=de9884b661b31e3b06274eb1c17fa5ea176b73d2f557a529d7e4333178b6391e =====
import { describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';

import {
  buildVisibleMarkerReviewBubbleMapSignature,
  buildVisibleMarkerReviewBubbleRenderToken,
  buildVisibleRestaurantIdSignature,
  wrapNaverMarkerContentWithReviewBubble,
  type VisibleMarkerReviewBubble,
} from '../lib/visible-marker-review-bubbles';

const baseBubble: VisibleMarkerReviewBubble = {
  restaurantId: 'restaurant-1',
  reviewId: 'review-1',
  userName: '리뷰어',
  content: '국물이 진하고 만두가 푸짐해요.',
  photoUrl: 'https://example.com/review.jpg',
};

describe('visible marker review bubble signatures', () => {
  test('in-place review field updates reach the wrapped HTML', () => {
    const bubble = { ...baseBubble, photoUrl: null as string | null };
    const marker = '<div data-testid="marker">식당</div>';
    expect(wrapNaverMarkerContentWithReviewBubble(marker, bubble, false)).toContain(bubble.content);
    bubble.content = '제자리에서 갱신한 리뷰';
    expect(wrapNaverMarkerContentWithReviewBubble(marker, bubble, false)).toContain(bubble.content);
    bubble.userName = '새 리뷰 작성자';
    expect(wrapNaverMarkerContentWithReviewBubble(marker, bubble, false)).toContain(bubble.userName);
    bubble.photoUrl = 'https://example.com/replaced-photo.webp';
    expect(wrapNaverMarkerContentWithReviewBubble(marker, bubble, false)).toContain(bubble.photoUrl);
  });
  test('reuses the id signature for the same restaurant list', () => {
    const restaurants = [{ id: 'a' }, { id: 'b' }];
    const first = buildVisibleRestaurantIdSignature(restaurants);
    expect(buildVisibleRestaurantIdSignature(restaurants)).toBe(first);
    expect(first).toBe('a|b');
    expect(buildVisibleRestaurantIdSignature([{ id: 'a' }, { id: 'b' }])).toBe('a|b');
  });

  test('changes when rendered bubble content changes under the same review id', () => {
    const baseSignature = buildVisibleMarkerReviewBubbleMapSignature({
      [baseBubble.restaurantId]: baseBubble,
    });

    for (const changedBubble of [
      { ...baseBubble, userName: '다른 리뷰어' },
      { ...baseBubble, content: '새로 동기화된 리뷰 내용' },
      { ...baseBubble, photoUrl: 'https://example.com/updated-review.jpg' },
      { ...baseBubble, photoUrl: null },
    ]) {
      expect(buildVisibleMarkerReviewBubbleMapSignature({
        [changedBubble.restaurantId]: changedBubble,
      })).not.toBe(baseSignature);
    }
  });

  test('is stable regardless of record insertion order', () => {
    const secondBubble: VisibleMarkerReviewBubble = {
      ...baseBubble,
      restaurantId: 'restaurant-2',
      reviewId: 'review-2',
    };

    expect(buildVisibleMarkerReviewBubbleMapSignature({
      [baseBubble.restaurantId]: baseBubble,
      [secondBubble.restaurantId]: secondBubble,
    })).toBe(buildVisibleMarkerReviewBubbleMapSignature({
      [secondBubble.restaurantId]: secondBubble,
      [baseBubble.restaurantId]: baseBubble,
    }));
  });

  test('reuses one bubble render token until its fields change', () => {
    const bubble = { ...baseBubble };
    const first = buildVisibleMarkerReviewBubbleRenderToken(bubble, false);
    expect(buildVisibleMarkerReviewBubbleRenderToken(bubble, false)).toBe(first);
    expect(buildVisibleMarkerReviewBubbleRenderToken(bubble, true)).not.toBe(first);
    bubble.content = '다른 내용';
    expect(buildVisibleMarkerReviewBubbleRenderToken(bubble, false)).not.toBe(first);
  });
});

===== BEGIN FILE apps/web/lib/naver-map-cluster-visuals.ts bytes=5849 sha256=e6827ce69615a202ca866aa5ced193b78d8b16c3ab9f44d3abb665e7b94f4d92 =====
import type { ClusterFeature } from 'supercluster';
import { createClusterMarkerHTML } from '@/lib/cluster-marker';
import type { ClusterProperties } from '@/lib/clustering';
import { publicPopularityTier } from '@/lib/evidence-engineering/popularity-marker';

const clusterVisualKeyCache = new Map<string, number>();

export function getClusterVisualKey(uniqueKey: string | number) {
    if (typeof uniqueKey === 'number') return uniqueKey;
    const cached = clusterVisualKeyCache.get(uniqueKey);
    if (cached !== undefined) return cached;

    const hash = Math.abs(uniqueKey.split('').reduce((acc, value) => (acc * 31 + value.charCodeAt(0)) | 0, 0));
    clusterVisualKeyCache.set(uniqueKey, hash);
    return hash;
}

export function buildClusterMarkerFeature({
    count,
    lat,
    lng,
}: {
    count: number;
    lat: number;
    lng: number;
}): ClusterFeature<ClusterProperties> {
    return {
        properties: { point_count: count },
        geometry: { coordinates: [lng, lat] },
    } as unknown as ClusterFeature<ClusterProperties>;
}

const clusterMarkerHtmlCache = new Map<string, string>();
const categoryJoinCache = new WeakMap<string[], string>();
const CLUSTER_MARKER_HTML_CACHE_LIMIT = 256;

function joinedClusterCategories(categories: string[]) {
    const cached = categoryJoinCache.get(categories);
    if (cached !== undefined) return cached;
    const joined = categories.join('\n');
    categoryJoinCache.set(categories, joined);
    return joined;
}

export function buildClusterMarkerContent({
    categories,
    count,
    currentIndex,
    lat,
    lng,
    popularityTier,
}: {
    categories: string[];
    count: number;
    currentIndex: number;
    lat: number;
    lng: number;
    popularityTier?: ClusterProperties["popularityTier"];
}) {
    const cacheKey = `${count}:${currentIndex}:${publicPopularityTier(popularityTier)}:${joinedClusterCategories(categories)}`;
    const cached = clusterMarkerHtmlCache.get(cacheKey);
    if (cached) return cached;

    const html = createClusterMarkerHTML(
        buildClusterMarkerFeature({ count, lat, lng }),
        categories,
        currentIndex,
        popularityTier,
    );
    if (clusterMarkerHtmlCache.size >= CLUSTER_MARKER_HTML_CACHE_LIMIT) {
        const oldest = clusterMarkerHtmlCache.keys().next().value;
        if (oldest !== undefined) clusterMarkerHtmlCache.delete(oldest);
    }
    clusterMarkerHtmlCache.set(cacheKey, html);
    return html;
}

const CLUSTER_MARKER_ANCHOR = { x: 24, y: 24 };
const clusterVisualCache = new WeakMap<string[], Map<number, Map<string, { content: string; anchor: { x: number; y: number } }>>>();

export function getNaverClusterMarkerVisual({
    categories,
    count,
    currentIndex,
    lat,
    lng,
    popularityTier,
}: {
    categories: string[];
    count: number;
    currentIndex: number;
    lat: number;
    lng: number;
    popularityTier?: ClusterProperties["popularityTier"];
}) {
    let byCount = clusterVisualCache.get(categories);
    if (!byCount) {
        byCount = new Map();
        clusterVisualCache.set(categories, byCount);
    }
    let byIndex = byCount.get(count);
    if (!byIndex) {
        byIndex = new Map();
        byCount.set(count, byIndex);
    }
    const visualKey = `${currentIndex}:${publicPopularityTier(popularityTier)}`;
    const cached = byIndex.get(visualKey);
    if (cached) return cached;

    const visual = {
        content: buildClusterMarkerContent({ categories, count, currentIndex, lat, lng, popularityTier }),
        anchor: CLUSTER_MARKER_ANCHOR,
    };
    byIndex.set(visualKey, visual);
    return visual;
}

const clusterPlanCache = new WeakMap<object, {
    anchor: { x: number; y: number };
    content: string;
    position: { lat: number; lng: number };
}>();

export function buildNaverClusterMarkerRenderPlan({
    categories,
    count,
    currentIndex,
    popularityTier,
    position,
}: {
    categories: string[];
    count: number;
    currentIndex: number;
    popularityTier?: ClusterProperties["popularityTier"];
    position: { lat: number; lng: number };
}) {
    const visual = getNaverClusterMarkerVisual({
        categories,
        count,
        currentIndex,
        lat: position.lat,
        lng: position.lng,
        popularityTier,
    });
    const cachedPlan = clusterPlanCache.get(visual);
    if (
        cachedPlan &&
        cachedPlan.position.lat === position.lat &&
        cachedPlan.position.lng === position.lng
    ) {
        return cachedPlan;
    }

    const plan = {
        anchor: visual.anchor,
        content: visual.content,
        position,
    };
    clusterPlanCache.set(visual, plan);
    return plan;
}

export function buildNaverClusterAnimationIconPlan({
    categories,
    count,
    getCurrentIndex,
    popularityTier,
    position,
    uniqueKey,
}: {
    categories: string[];
    count: number;
    getCurrentIndex: (hash: number, categoryCount: number) => number;
    popularityTier?: ClusterProperties["popularityTier"];
    position: { lat: number; lng: number };
    uniqueKey: string | number;
}) {
    const hash = getClusterVisualKey(uniqueKey);
    const currentIndex = getCurrentIndex(hash, categories.length);
    const renderPlan = buildNaverClusterMarkerRenderPlan({
        categories,
        count,
        currentIndex,
        popularityTier,
        position,
    });

    return {
        ...renderPlan,
        currentIndex,
        hash,
    } as const;
}

export function shouldReplaceNaverMarkerIcon(
    current: { content?: unknown; anchor?: { x?: number; y?: number } | null } | null | undefined,
    next: { content: unknown; anchor: { x: number; y: number } },
): boolean {
    if (!current || current.content !== next.content) return true;
    return current.anchor?.x !== next.anchor.x || current.anchor?.y !== next.anchor.y;
}

import { afterAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';

import { MarkerPool, markerPool } from '../lib/marker-pool';

type Position = {
  id: string;
  equals: (other: unknown) => boolean;
};

type Icon = {
  content?: unknown;
  anchor?: { x: number; y: number } | null;
};

const makePosition = (id: string): Position => ({
  id,
  equals: (other: unknown) => (other as { id?: string } | null)?.id === id,
});

class FakeMarker {
  map: unknown;
  position: unknown;
  icon: Icon;
  zIndex = 0;
  clickListener?: (event: unknown) => void;
  setMapCalls = 0;
  setPositionCalls = 0;
  setIconCalls = 0;
  setZIndexCalls = 0;
  readonly classes = new Set<string>();
  readonly element = {
    classList: {
      add: (...names: string[]) => names.forEach((name) => this.classes.add(name)),
      remove: (...names: string[]) => names.forEach((name) => this.classes.delete(name)),
    },
    style: { opacity: '' },
  };

  constructor(options: { position: unknown; icon: Icon; map: unknown }) {
    this.position = options.position;
    this.icon = options.icon;
    this.map = options.map;
    createdMarkers.push(this);
  }

  getMap() {
    return this.map;
  }

  setMap(map: unknown | null) {
    this.map = map;
    this.setMapCalls += 1;
  }

  getPosition() {
    return this.position as Position;
  }

  setPosition(position: unknown) {
    this.position = position;
    this.setPositionCalls += 1;
  }

  getIcon() {
    return this.icon;
  }

  setIcon(icon: Icon) {
    this.icon = icon;
    this.setIconCalls += 1;
  }

  getElement() {
    return this.element as unknown as HTMLElement;
  }

  setZIndex(zIndex: number) {
    this.zIndex = zIndex;
    this.setZIndexCalls += 1;
  }
}

const createdMarkers: FakeMarker[] = [];
let listenerRegistrations = 0;
let listenerRemovals = 0;
const originalWindowDescriptor = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(globalThis, 'window');

function installFakeNaverMaps() {
  Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'window', {
    configurable: true,
    writable: true,
    value: {
      naver: {
        maps: {
          Marker: FakeMarker,
          Event: {
            addListener: (marker: FakeMarker, event: string, listener: (event: unknown) => void) => {
              expect(event).toBe('click');
              marker.clickListener = listener;
              listenerRegistrations += 1;
              return { marker, listener };
            },
            removeListener: (handle: { marker: FakeMarker; listener: (event: unknown) => void }) => {
              if (handle.marker.clickListener === handle.listener) handle.marker.clickListener = undefined;
              listenerRemovals += 1;
            },
          },
        },
      },
    },
  });
}

function captureTimeouts() {
  const originalSetTimeout = globalThis.setTimeout;
  const originalClearTimeout = globalThis.clearTimeout;
  const callbacks: Array<() => void> = [];
  const cleared = new Set<number>();
  globalThis.setTimeout = ((callback: TimerHandler) => {
    callbacks.push(() => (callback as () => void)());
    return callbacks.length as unknown as ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;
  }) as typeof setTimeout;
  globalThis.clearTimeout = ((handle: unknown) => {
    if (typeof handle === 'number') cleared.add(handle);
  }) as typeof clearTimeout;

  return {
    callbacks,
    cleared,
    flush() {
      callbacks.forEach((callback, index) => {
        if (!cleared.has(index + 1)) callback();
      });
      callbacks.length = 0;
    },
    restore() {
      globalThis.setTimeout = originalSetTimeout;
      globalThis.clearTimeout = originalClearTimeout;
    },
  };
}

installFakeNaverMaps();

beforeEach(() => {
  installFakeNaverMaps();
  markerPool.clear();
  markerPool.resetStats();
  createdMarkers.length = 0;
  listenerRegistrations = 0;
  listenerRemovals = 0;
});

afterAll(() => {
  markerPool.clear();
  markerPool.resetStats();
  if (originalWindowDescriptor) {
    Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'window', originalWindowDescriptor);
  } else {
    delete (globalThis as { window?: unknown }).window;
  }
});

describe('marker pool', () => {
  test('failed provider detachment is discarded and does not strand clear or reuse a corrupt marker', () => {
    const broken = markerPool.acquire('broken', makePosition('a'), { content: 'a' }, 'map');
    const valid = markerPool.acquire('valid', makePosition('b'), { content: 'b' }, 'map');
    broken.setMap = () => { throw new Error('NAVER_MARKER_DETACH_FAILED'); };
    expect(() => markerPool.release('broken')).not.toThrow();
    expect(markerPool.getStats()).toMatchObject({ activeCount: 1, poolSize: 0 });
    const next = markerPool.acquire('new', makePosition('c'), { content: 'c' }, 'map');
    expect(next).not.toBe(broken);
    next.setMap = () => { throw new Error('NAVER_MARKER_DETACH_FAILED'); };
    expect(() => markerPool.clear()).not.toThrow();
    expect(valid.getMap()).toBe(null);
    expect(markerPool.getStats()).toMatchObject({ activeCount: 0, poolSize: 0 });
  });

  test('provider listener teardown failure does not strand the remaining markers or pool state', () => {
    const markers = ['teardown-a', 'teardown-b'].map(id =>
      markerPool.acquire(id, makePosition(id), { content: id }, 'map'),
    );
    let attempts = 0;
    const events = window.naver.maps.Event;
    const remove = events.removeListener;
    events.removeListener = () => {
      attempts += 1;
      throw new TypeError('PROVIDER_LISTENER_CLEANUP_FAILED');
    };
    try {
      expect(() => markerPool.clear()).not.toThrow();
      expect(attempts).toBe(2);
      expect(markers.every(marker => marker.getMap() === null)).toBe(true);
      expect(markerPool.getStats()).toMatchObject({ activeCount: 0, poolSize: 0 });
      markerPool.clear();
      expect(attempts).toBe(2);
    } finally {
      events.removeListener = remove;
    }
  });

  test('exports the same singleton and creates one marker/listener for a new id', () => {
    expect(MarkerPool.getInstance()).toBe(markerPool);
    const onClick = () => undefined;
    const marker = markerPool.acquire(
      'restaurant-1',
      makePosition('p1'),
      { content: 'icon-1', anchor: { x: 10, y: 20 } },
      'map-1',
      onClick,
    ) as unknown as FakeMarker;

    expect(createdMarkers).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(listenerRegistrations).toBe(1);
    expect(markerPool.get('restaurant-1')).toBe(marker as never);
    expect(markerPool.has('restaurant-1')).toBe(true);
    expect(marker.getMap()).toBe('map-1');
    expect(markerPool.getStats()).toEqual({
      created: 1,
      reused: 0,
      released: 0,
      activeCount: 1,
      poolSize: 0,
      hitRate: 0,
    });
  });

  test('reuses an active marker and updates only changed map, position, icon, and click handler', () => {
    let firstClicks = 0;
    let secondClicks = 0;
    const first = markerPool.acquire(
      'restaurant-1',
      makePosition('p1'),
      { content: 'icon-1', anchor: { x: 10, y: 20 } },
      'map-1',
      () => { firstClicks += 1; },
    ) as unknown as FakeMarker;

    const unchanged = markerPool.acquire(
      'restaurant-1',
      makePosition('p1'),
      { content: 'icon-1', anchor: { x: 10, y: 20 } },
      'map-1',
      () => { secondClicks += 1; },
    ) as unknown as FakeMarker;

    expect(unchanged).toBe(first);
    expect(first.setMapCalls).toBe(0);
    expect(first.setPositionCalls).toBe(0);
    expect(first.setIconCalls).toBe(0);
    first.clickListener?.({});
    expect(firstClicks).toBe(0);
    expect(secondClicks).toBe(1);

    markerPool.acquire(
      'restaurant-1',
      makePosition('p2'),
      { content: 'icon-2', anchor: { x: 11, y: 20 } },
      'map-2',
    );
    expect(first.setMapCalls).toBe(1);
    expect(first.setPositionCalls).toBe(1);
    expect(first.setIconCalls).toBe(1);
    expect(markerPool.getStats().created).toBe(1);
    expect(markerPool.getStats().reused).toBe(0);
  });

  test('releases after the fade delay and reuses the pooled marker for a new id', () => {
    const timers = captureTimeouts();
    try {
      const marker = markerPool.acquire(
        'restaurant-1',
        makePosition('p1'),
        { content: 'icon-1', anchor: null },
        'map-1',
      ) as unknown as FakeMarker;

      markerPool.release('restaurant-1');
      expect(markerPool.has('restaurant-1')).toBe(false);
      expect(marker.classes.has('marker-fade-out')).toBe(false);
      expect(marker.getMap()).toBeNull();
      expect(markerPool.getStats()).toMatchObject({ released: 1, poolSize: 1 });

      const reused = markerPool.acquire(
        'restaurant-2',
        makePosition('p2'),
        { content: 'icon-2', anchor: null },
        'map-2',
      ) as unknown as FakeMarker;
      expect(reused).toBe(marker);
      expect(createdMarkers).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(listenerRegistrations).toBe(1);
      expect(markerPool.getStats()).toMatchObject({ created: 1, reused: 1, activeCount: 1, poolSize: 0 });
      expect(markerPool.getStats().hitRate).toBe(0.5);
    } finally {
      timers.restore();
    }
  });

  test('reclaims a fading marker instead of creating a second node', () => {
    const timers = captureTimeouts();
    try {
      const marker = markerPool.acquire(
        'restaurant-1',
        makePosition('p1'),
        { content: 'icon-1', anchor: null },
        'map-1',
      ) as unknown as FakeMarker;

      markerPool.release('restaurant-1');
      expect(marker.getMap()).toBeNull();
      const reclaimed = markerPool.acquire(
        'restaurant-1',
        makePosition('p1'),
        { content: 'icon-1', anchor: null },
        'map-1',

      expect(reclaimed).toBe(marker);
      expect(createdMarkers).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(marker.classes.has('marker-fade-out')).toBe(false);
      expect(marker.getMap()).toBe('map-1');

  });

  test('updates active markers and treats unknown ids as safe no-ops', () => {
    const marker = markerPool.acquire(

      makePosition('p1'),
      { content: 'icon-1' },
      'map-1',
    ) as unknown as FakeMarker;

    markerPool.update('missing', {
      position: makePosition('missing'),
      icon: { content: 'missing' },

    });
    markerPool.release('missing');
    expect(marker.setPositionCalls).toBe(0);
    expect(marker.setIconCalls).toBe(0);
    expect(marker.setZIndexCalls).toBe(0);

    markerPool.update('restaurant-1', {
      position: makePosition('p2'),
      icon: { content: 'icon-2' },
      zIndex: 12,
    });
    expect(marker.setPositionCalls).toBe(1);
    expect(marker.setIconCalls).toBe(1);
    expect(marker.setZIndexCalls).toBe(1);

  });

  test('a returning batch reuses its restaurant identities and updated click handlers without rebuilding icons', () => {
    const ids = ['restaurant-a', 'restaurant-b', 'restaurant-c'];
    const clicks: string[] = [];
    const original = ids.map(id => markerPool.acquire(id, makePosition(id), { content: id }, 'map', () => clicks.push('stale')));
    markerPool.releaseAll();

    ids.forEach((id, index) => {
      const marker = markerPool.acquire(id, makePosition(id), { content: id }, 'map', () => clicks.push(id)) as unknown as FakeMarker;
      expect(marker).toBe(original[index]);
      expect(marker.setIconCalls).toBe(0);
      expect(marker.setPositionCalls).toBe(0);
      marker.clickListener?.({});
    });
    expect(clicks).toEqual(ids);
    expect(markerPool.getStats()).toMatchObject({ created: 3, reused: 3, activeCount: 3, poolSize: 0 });

    markerPool.releaseAll();
    const changed = markerPool.acquire('new-restaurant', makePosition('new-position'), { content: 'new-icon' }, 'map') as unknown as FakeMarker;
    expect(changed.getPosition().id).toBe('new-position');
    expect(changed.getIcon().content).toBe('new-icon');
    expect(changed.setIconCalls).toBe(1);
  });

  test('releaseExcept and releaseAll keep only the requested active ids before delayed cleanup', () => {
    const timers = captureTimeouts();
    try {
      for (const id of ['a', 'b', 'c']) {
        markerPool.acquire(id, makePosition(id), { content: id }, 'map');
      }

      markerPool.releaseExcept(new Set(['b']));
      expect(markerPool.has('a')).toBe(false);
      expect(markerPool.has('b')).toBe(true);
      expect(markerPool.has('c')).toBe(false);
      expect(markerPool.getStats().activeCount).toBe(1);

      markerPool.releaseAll();
      expect(markerPool.getStats().activeCount).toBe(0);
      expect(markerPool.getStats()).toMatchObject({ released: 3, poolSize: 3 });
    } finally {
      timers.restore();
    }
  });

  test('clear cancels a release that was already scheduled', () => {
    const timers = captureTimeouts();
    try {
      markerPool.acquire('restaurant-1', makePosition('p1'), { content: 'icon' }, 'map');
      markerPool.release('restaurant-1');

      markerPool.clear();
      expect(markerPool.getStats().poolSize).toBe(0);
      expect(markerPool.getStats().activeCount).toBe(0);
      expect(markerPool.getStats().released).toBe(1);

      timers.flush();
      expect(markerPool.getStats().poolSize).toBe(0);
      expect(markerPool.getStats().released).toBe(1);
    } finally {
      timers.restore();
    }
  });

  test('clear leaves the pool empty for markers that were still active', () => {
    const timers = captureTimeouts();
    try {
      markerPool.acquire('restaurant-9', makePosition('p9'), { content: 'icon' }, 'map');

      markerPool.clear();
      timers.flush();

      expect(markerPool.getStats()).toMatchObject({ activeCount: 0, poolSize: 0 });
    } finally {
      timers.restore();
    }
  });

test('pool reuse and changed visit badges update static marker content even with the same image', () => {
  const originalDocument = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(globalThis, 'document');
  try {
    for (const scenario of [
      { currentId: 'restaurant-old', nextId: 'restaurant-new', currentBadge: '', nextBadge: '' },
      { currentId: 'restaurant-same', nextId: 'restaurant-same', currentBadge: '<span>2 visits</span>', nextBadge: '<span>3 visits</span>' },
    ]) {
      markerPool.clear();
      const node = (id: string, badge: string) => ({
        getAttribute: (attribute: string) => attribute === 'style' ? 'width:32px' : attribute === 'data-restaurant-id' ? id : null,
        querySelector: (selector: string) => selector === 'img'
          ? { getAttribute: () => '/marker.webp' }
          : selector.includes('visit-count') && badge ? { outerHTML: badge } : null,
        parentElement: null,
        querySelectorAll: () => [],
        insertBefore: () => { throw new Error('Static content must use SDK icon replacement'); },
      });
      const current = node(scenario.currentId, scenario.currentBadge);
      const next = node(scenario.nextId, scenario.nextBadge);
      Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'document', {
        configurable: true,
        value: { createElement: () => ({ innerHTML: '', content: { querySelector: (selector: string) => selector === '[data-testid="marker"]' ? next : null } }) },
      });
      const marker = markerPool.acquire(scenario.currentId, makePosition('old'), { content: 'old icon' }, 'map') as unknown as FakeMarker;
      marker.getElement = () => ({ querySelector: () => current }) as unknown as HTMLElement;
      markerPool.release(scenario.currentId);
      const newContent = `<div data-testid="marker" data-restaurant-id="${scenario.nextId}">${scenario.nextBadge}<img src="/marker.webp"></div>`;
      const reused = markerPool.acquire(scenario.nextId, makePosition('new'), { content: newContent }, 'map') as unknown as FakeMarker;
      expect(reused).toBe(marker);
      expect(reused.setIconCalls).toBe(1);
      expect(reused.getIcon().content).toBe(newContent);
      expect(markerPool.has(scenario.currentId)).toBe(scenario.currentId === scenario.nextId);
      expect(markerPool.has(scenario.nextId)).toBe(true);
    }
  } finally {
    if (originalDocument) Object.defineProperty(globalThis, 'document', originalDocument);
    else delete (globalThis as { document?: unknown }).document;
  }
});


test('detached SDK clicks cannot dispatch stale data and reuse keeps one owned listener', () => {
  let oldClicks = 0;
  let currentClicks = 0;
  const first = markerPool.acquire('old-place', makePosition('p1'), { content: 'old' }, 'map', () => oldClicks++) as unknown as FakeMarker;
  markerPool.release('old-place');
  first.clickListener?.({});
  expect(oldClicks).toBe(0);
  const next = markerPool.acquire('new-place', makePosition('p2'), { content: 'new' }, 'map', () => currentClicks++) as unknown as FakeMarker;
  expect(next).toBe(first);
  next.clickListener?.({});
  expect(currentClicks).toBe(1);
  expect(listenerRegistrations).toBe(1);
  markerPool.clear();
  expect(next.clickListener).toBeUndefined();
  expect(listenerRemovals).toBe(1);
  markerPool.clear();
  expect(listenerRemovals).toBe(1);
});

test('disposal stays bounded across cache capacity and repeated clear', () => {
  for (let i = 0; i < 1002; i++) markerPool.acquire(`capacity-${i}`, makePosition(`p-${i}`), { content: `icon-${i}` }, 'map');
  markerPool.releaseAll();
  expect(markerPool.getStats().poolSize).toBe(1000);
  expect(listenerRemovals).toBe(2);
  markerPool.clear();
  expect(listenerRemovals).toBe(1002);
  markerPool.clear();
  expect(listenerRemovals).toBe(1002);
});

import { afterAll, beforeEach, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
import { syncRetainedMarkerAccessibility } from '../lib/retained-marker-accessibility';

class AppMarkerElement {
    attributes = new Map<string, string>();
    matches(selector: string) { return selector === '[data-testid="marker"]'; }
    querySelector() { return null; }
    closest() { return null; }
    getAttribute(name: string) { return this.attributes.get(name) ?? null; }
    setAttribute(name: string, value: string) { this.attributes.set(name, value); }
    removeAttribute(name: string) { this.attributes.delete(name); }
}
function fixture(root: AppMarkerElement | null) {
    return { getElement: () => root as unknown as HTMLElement | null, __tzudongRetainedMarkerAriaHidden: undefined as boolean | undefined };
}
const savedObserver = globalThis.MutationObserver;
class FakeObserver {
    static instances: FakeObserver[] = [];
    disconnected = false;
    constructor(private callback: MutationCallback) { FakeObserver.instances.push(this); }
    observe() {}
    disconnect() { this.disconnected = true; }
    fire() { if (!this.disconnected) this.callback([], this as unknown as MutationObserver); }
}
beforeEach(() => {
    FakeObserver.instances = [];
    globalThis.MutationObserver = FakeObserver as unknown as typeof MutationObserver;
});
afterAll(() => { globalThis.MutationObserver = savedObserver; });
test('outside-padded mask is owned, reversible and has no geometry or focus changes', () => {
    const root = new AppMarkerElement();root.setAttribute('role','button');root.setAttribute('style','width:28px');
    const marker = fixture(root);
    syncRetainedMarkerAccessibility(marker,false);
    expect(root.getAttribute('aria-hidden')).toBe('true');
    expect(root.getAttribute('data-tzudong-retained-marker-hidden')).toBe('true');
    expect(root.getAttribute('role')).toBe('button');expect(root.getAttribute('style')).toBe('width:28px');
    expect(root.getAttribute('tabindex')).toBeNull();
    syncRetainedMarkerAccessibility(marker,true);
    expect(root.getAttribute('aria-hidden')).toBeNull();expect(root.getAttribute('data-tzudong-retained-marker-hidden')).toBeNull();
    expect(marker.__tzudongRetainedMarkerAriaHidden).toBe(false);
});
test('does not remove a hidden state owned by another source', () => {

    expect(root.getAttribute('aria-hidden')).toBe('true');

import { describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';

import {
    buildNaverClusterAnimationIconPlan,
    buildClusterMarkerContent,

    buildNaverClusterMarkerRenderPlan,
    getClusterVisualKey,

    shouldReplaceNaverMarkerIcon,

describe('naver map cluster visuals', () => {
    test('hashes string keys deterministically', () => {
        expect(getClusterVisualKey('gangnam')).toBe(getClusterVisualKey('gangnam'));
        expect(getClusterVisualKey(42)).toBe(42);
    });

    test('builds cluster feature with lng/lat coordinates', () => {
        const feature = buildClusterMarkerFeature({ count: 3, lat: 37.5, lng: 127.0 });
        expect(feature.properties.point_count).toBe(3);
        expect(feature.geometry.coordinates).toEqual([127.0, 37.5]);
    });

    test('builds cluster marker html content', () => {
        const html = buildClusterMarkerContent({
            categories: ['한식', '분식'],
            count: 4,
            currentIndex: 0,
            lat: 37.5,
            lng: 127.0,
        });
        expect(html).toContain('4');
        expect(html).toContain('/images/maker-images/webp/korean.webp');
        expect(html).toContain('/images/maker-images/korean.png');
        expect(html).toContain('type="image/webp"');
        const categories = ['한식', '분식'];
        const first = buildClusterMarkerContent({
            categories,
            count: 4,
            currentIndex: 0,
            lat: 37.5,
            lng: 127.0,
        });
        expect(buildClusterMarkerContent({
            categories,
            count: 4,
            currentIndex: 0,
            lat: 37.51,
            lng: 127.01,
        })).toBe(first);
    });

    test('returns naver cluster marker visual payload', () => {
        const visual = getNaverClusterMarkerVisual({
            categories: ['한식'],
            count: 3,
            currentIndex: 0,
            lat: 37.5,
            lng: 127.0,
        });
        expect(visual.anchor).toEqual({ x: 24, y: 24 });
        expect(visual.content).toContain('/images/maker-images/webp/korean.webp');
        expect(visual.content).toContain('/images/maker-images/korean.png');
        const categories = ['한식'];
        const first = getNaverClusterMarkerVisual({
            categories,
            count: 3,
            currentIndex: 0,
            lat: 37.5,
            lng: 127.0,
        });
        expect(getNaverClusterMarkerVisual({
            categories,
            count: 3,
            currentIndex: 0,
            lat: 37.6,
            lng: 127.2,
        })).toBe(first);
    });

    test('builds cluster marker render plan from position and current animation index', () => {
        const plan = buildNaverClusterMarkerRenderPlan({
            categories: ['분식'],
            count: 5,
            currentIndex: 0,
            position: { lat: 37.6, lng: 127.1 },
        });

        expect(plan.position).toEqual({ lat: 37.6, lng: 127.1 });
        const categories = ['분식'];
        const first = buildNaverClusterMarkerRenderPlan({
            categories,
            count: 5,
            currentIndex: 0,
            position: { lat: 37.6, lng: 127.1 },
        });
        expect(buildNaverClusterMarkerRenderPlan({
            categories,
            count: 5,
            currentIndex: 0,
            position: { lat: 37.6, lng: 127.1 },

        expect(plan.anchor).toEqual({ x: 24, y: 24 });
        expect(plan.content).toContain('5');
        expect(plan.content).toContain('/images/maker-images/webp/snack_bar.webp');
        expect(plan.content).toContain('/images/maker-images/snack_bar.png');
    });

    test('builds animation icon plan from key and injected index resolver', () => {
        const plan = buildNaverClusterAnimationIconPlan({
            categories: ['한식'],
            count: 6,
            getCurrentIndex: (hash, categoryCount) => {
                expect(hash).toBe(getClusterVisualKey('seoul'));
                expect(categoryCount).toBe(1);
                return 0;
            },
            position: { lat: 37.55, lng: 126.98 },
            uniqueKey: 'seoul',
        });

        expect(plan.hash).toBe(getClusterVisualKey('seoul'));
        expect(plan.currentIndex).toBe(0);
        expect(plan.position).toEqual({ lat: 37.55, lng: 126.98 });
        expect(plan.anchor).toEqual({ x: 24, y: 24 });
        expect(plan.content).toContain('6');
        expect(plan.content).toContain('/images/maker-images/webp/korean.webp');
        expect(plan.content).toContain('/images/maker-images/korean.png');
    });

    test('skips a marker icon write when the content and anchor are unchanged', () => {
        const icon = { content: '<div>same</div>', anchor: { x: 24, y: 24 } };
        expect(shouldReplaceNaverMarkerIcon(icon, icon)).toBe(false);
        expect(shouldReplaceNaverMarkerIcon(icon, { ...icon, content: '<div>next</div>' })).toBe(true);
        expect(shouldReplaceNaverMarkerIcon(null, icon)).toBe(true);
    });
});

===== BEGIN DOCUMENT docs/evidence/formal-map-invariants-20261023-v1/AUDIT.md bytes=11914 sha256=a99ecdd19415611bd64f2f8fb9318a5f385a3171db1db6d32f732c2435aba383 =====
# Tzudong 상태·수식 점검

8개 반례를 실제 코드로 재현하고 수정했다. **한정된 입력·순서에 대한 검사이며 전체 시스템의 무오류 증명이 아니다.** 새 운영 배포, 실제 지도 SDK와 PC·모바일·태블릿의 최종 프리뷰, Nightly 통과를 이 결과로 대신하지 않는다.

기준은 최신 develop `56427320a44fdd65a86dc49e7e63d80dd9935c2e`, 수정·검증 코드의 동기화 커밋은 `fe4b73dac510d4670517c7d3eca4f0c2470d4297`다. 원래 작업과 다른 소유자의 변경을 보존했다. 현재 브랜치는 `codex/formal-map-invariants-20261023`이다.

## 반례와 수정

| ID | 판정 | 실제 반례 / 영향 | 수정과 검사 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| FORMAL-01 | ACCEPT | 같은 마커의 이미지 URL 등이 같으면 부분 패치가 성공해 불 등급·SVG나 이미지 설명이 이전 값으로 남았다. | 말풍선을 제외한 **전체** 기존/다음 HTML이 같을 때만 부분 패치. 48종·객체 갱신 2방식의 9,216건의 활성/반환 후 재사용 전이, 실제 Chromium DOM. 말풍선만 바뀌면 이미지 노드는 계속 유지. |
| FORMAL-02 | ACCEPT | `1 / Number.MIN_VALUE` 및 큰 유한 gamma와 여러 방문의 곱이 `Infinity`가 되어 입력의 유한성만으로 결과를 보장할 수 없었다. | 반감기·방문 배수의 결과도 유한성 검사. 정상 계수의 결과는 유지. 두 guard를 각각 제거한 변형 모두 탐지. |
| FORMAL-03 | ACCEPT | 안전 정수인 최대 timestamp에 5,000ms를 더하면 안전 정수 범위를 벗어났다. | 샘플링 창의 끝도 동일한 timestamp 경계로 검사. 실제 함수를 통한 실패/정상 회귀 및 guard 제거 변형 탐지. |
| FORMAL-04 | ACCEPT | `(freshness, visit_multiplier)` 벡터를 scalar 점수로 환산한 정책이 없는데도 운영 점수가 존재하면 `differs=true`, 신호가 모두 있으면 쓰기 가능으로 표시했다. | 비교 미성립을 `comparison_present=false`, `differs=null`, `differ_count=null`로 표현하고 쓰기 차단. 호출자는 현재 내부 테스트뿐임을 검색 확인. 임의 점수식은 도입하지 않음. |
| FORMAL-05 | ACCEPT | metric의 `true`가 `Number(true)=1`, `[123]`이 `123`으로 변환되어 실제 관측 없는 불 등급을 부여할 수 있었다. | 기존 `safeCount` 경계 재사용. boolean·배열·객체 등은 결측으로 남기고 standard 유지. 기존 불필요한 중복 parser를 추가하지 않음. |
| FORMAL-06 | ACCEPT | 같은 video에 서로 다른 관측 수치가 있으면 마지막 행이 승리해 순서에 따라 결과가 달라졌다. | 충돌을 고정 코드로 거부. 두 입력 순서 모두 거부하고 동일 중복은 한 번만 사용. |
| FORMAL-07 | ACCEPT | 초기 모델은 리뷰를 이미지 안에 배치했지만 실제 Naver는 바깥 앵커에 둔다. 기존 리뷰 A→B 갱신이 기존 A를 남기고 B를 안쪽에 추가해 리뷰가 두 개가 됐다. | 실제 wrapper를 기존 리뷰 모듈로 이동해 Naver와 모델이 함께 사용. 기존 리뷰 노드를 제자리 교체하고 구조 변경은 SDK 아이콘 갱신. 수정 전 실제 래퍼에서 bubbles=2/currentReview=false를 재현했고 수정 후 한 개의 최신 리뷰·이미지 보존을 확인. |
| FORMAL-08 | ACCEPT | 리뷰 객체는 같고 content/userName/photoUrl만 바뀌면 wrapper 캐시가 예전 HTML을 반환했다. 기존 필드 감지 render token은 갱신을 알아도 wrapper 캐시는 참조·마커·화면 모드만 비교했다. | 이미 존재하는 필드별 render token을 wrapper 캐시에 연결. 같은 객체 갱신을 탐색에 추가해 202번째 조건의 stale review를 재현했고, 작성자·본문·사진 변경 native 회귀와 guard 제거 변형을 통과. |


초기 inline 리뷰 모델의 2,048건 통과는 실제 앵커 구조에 대한 검증이 아니었다. 이 correspondence 공백을 발견해 속성을 약화하지 않고 실제 wrapper를 모델에 연결했다. [추가 실패 원문](actual-wrapper-counterexample.txt)과 현재 9,216건 결과를 구분한다. 새 객체만 만들던 다음 모델도 동일 객체 갱신을 다루지 못했다. [동일 객체 캐시 반례](mutable-cache-counterexample.txt) 후 새/동일 객체 갱신 두 방식을 모두 탐색한다. 초기 원시 기록은 Git 커밋 `92bff943925941f047e244090c3ae3bc7615718b`에 그대로 남아 있다.

처음 소유권 모델에서 `A 표시 → A 반환 → 같은 객체로 B 표시 → 예전 A 변수로 클릭`을 A의 클릭으로 해석했다. 실제 객체는 이미 B를 표시한다. 이는 모델의 잘못된 소유권 가정으로 판정했고 제품 버그로 세지 않았다. 현재 모델은 물리 객체의 현재 소유자를 추적한다. 해제 후 아직 재사용되지 않은 객체의 클릭은 차단되어야 하며 guard 제거 변형으로 이 조건의 탐지력을 확인했다.

## 수식의 의미와 한계

`H(t)=1/(1+λt)`의 반감기는 `t=1/λ`다. `λ=0.0015=3/2000`이면 `2000/3 ≈ 666.6667일`이며 460일은 아니다. 460일에는 `100/169 ≈ 0.591716`이다. 기존 반박과 정상 결과를 유지했다.

`D(t)=f+(1-f)/(1+t/h)`에서 `0≤f<1`, `h>0`, `t≥0`이면 `D(0)=1`, `f≤D(t)≤1`이고 도함수는 `-(1-f)/(h(1+t/h)²)≤0`다. `D(h)=(1+f)/2`이므로 h는 **바닥을 뺀 가변 부분의 반감기**다. 전체 값이 0.5가 되는 시각이라는 해석은 일반적으로 성립하지 않는다. 부동소수점 구현에서 극단값의 반올림·underflow까지 실수체의 엄격 부등식으로 확대하지 않는다.

P98/P95/P85는 현행 builder가 포함하는 양의 유효 조회수 표본의 `ceil(pN)`번째 값을 문턱으로 삼는다. 동점이 없는 fire 수는 `N-ceil(0.98N)+1`이며 동점이면 더 많아질 수 있다. 모든 값이 같으면 모두 fire일 수도 있다. 따라서 정확히 상위 2%/5%/15% 인원 할당이라는 주장은 하지 않는다. 현재 JS 식과 정수 순위식을 N=1~5,000에서 비교한 결과는 [순위 계산 기록](rank-enumeration.json)에 있다. 이는 그 유한 범위의 식 비교이며 builder 전체나 모든 분포의 형식 증명은 아니다. 실제 builder에는 n=19/20 경계와 오염·중복 입력 검사가 있다.

방문 수는 고유 video ID만 센다. 수학적으로 유효한 실수 식과 JavaScript에서 표현 가능한 수는 다르므로 결과 overflow를 거부한다. 후보 신호와 운영 점수는 단위·합성 정책이 다르며, 데이터 존재만으로 수치 비교나 운영 변경 정당성을 증명하지 않는다.

## 상태전이와 검증

[상태전이도 Mermaid 원본](state-transitions.mmd)은 **마커 객체 한 개**의 요청 반환 시점 상태를 투영한다. 실제 풀은 공유되므로 두 식당의 재사용·해제를 함께 탐색했다. 그림과 체크가 같다는 사실은 실제 SDK의 refinement 증명이 아니다.

실행 명령은 `apps/web`에서 `bun scripts/verify-marker-formal.mjs --out /tmp/tzudong-formal-check.json`이다. [프로젝트 재검증 스킬](../../../.agents/skills/tzudong-formal-map-check/SKILL.md)을 한 번 실제로 따라 실행했고, [원시 검사·변형 결과](native-and-mutation-checks.json)에 보존했다.

- Bun 1.4.0, 실제 관측 Chromium 156.0.8078.47. 프로젝트 기본 Node 24 및 고정 native/compat compiler도 별도 확인.
- 마커 DOM 전이 9,216건: 4등급 × 2이미지 설명 × 2방문 배지 × 3리뷰 상태 = 48종. 모든 순서쌍 × 활성/반환 후 재사용 × 새/동일 리뷰 객체 갱신 두 방식.
- 8행동, 최대 깊이 4: 4,681 trace, 18,056 step. 같은 요청 반환 경계의 현재 콜백 소유권과 활성 수 확인.
- 검증 기반 원본 검사 통과, 의도적 오류 변형 8종 탐지, 원본 파일 hash 유지 및 복원 검사 통과. 실제 버그의 사전 실패 기록도 별도 보존.
- 관련 회귀 검사 86개 통과. 최종 verifier 자체의 baseline은 5파일 19검사, 복원 검사는 2파일 6검사다. 반복 실행 결과를 합쳐 검사 수를 부풀리지 않는다.
- scoped lint 통과. native/compat parity는 진단 0건, 논리 입력 3,382개. 새 도구의 초기 TS 파일은 Bun 타입 선언이 없는 프로젝트 설정과 충돌해 실패했고, 기존 스크립트 방식인 `.mjs`로 정리한 후 통과했다. 의존성이나 전역 타입 설정은 바꾸지 않았다.

미포함: 실제 사진이 있는 리뷰 UI 변형·픽셀 배치, 실제 Naver SDK, 네트워크, SDK의 동기 재진입, 무한 실행, fairness/진행성, 실제 하드웨어, 메모리·field·flicker·측정 성능, strict marker/CSP, 전체 식당 신원 증명. SDK 해제 실패 시 객체 폐기와 클릭 권한 정리는 다루지만 provider 내부 시각·자원 해제 성공은 주장하지 않는다.

## 외부 검토·배포·Nightly

기존 일반 추론 3단계 전문 91,596 bytes와 source hash는 [기존 감사 전달 기록](../marker-evidence-cross-audit-20261023-v1/AUDIT-DELIVERY.md)에 보존되어 있다. 새 8건은 그 보고서의 검토 대상 소스와 다른 delta이므로 이 문서만으로 외부 검토 완료라 하지 않는다. 사용자의 기존 Tzudong 한정 FIFO·슬롯 예외 승인을 재사용했으나, canonical `coordinate.py reserve`의 `dispatch_pending` 직렬화 gate는 선행 전송의 실제 answering 확인 전에는 FIFO·슬롯 예외만으로 통과되지 않았다. 현행 정책의 특정 타 소유 전송 1건에 대한 별도 예외는 그 전송을 명시한 직접 사용자 승인이 필요하며 이번 실행에서 요청하거나 사용하지 않았다. [대기열 체크포인트](external-review-checkpoint.json)의 이전 두 미전송 로컬 계획만 소유자가 withdraw 처리했다. 원격 작업 취소는 없었으며 최신 소스의 단일 delta 요청 `86eb51ad0f69dcedd3c5dd33`는 `queued`이고 업로드·전송은 0회다. 실제 kss2740 UI에서 4 Argon 최고 표시 높음/매우 철저함, Deep Research OFF, 현재 창 0%·주간 8%를 관측했지만 이는 관측 시점의 사실이며 전송 시점 가용성으로 재사용하지 않는다. 선행 전송은 해당 소유자가 실제 answering으로 대조해야 한다. 허용될 때 같은 요청의 신선한 모드·한도·소스 접근을 확인하고 Gemini delta 전문→소유 release→유효 지적 해결→실제 GPT-6 Pro 역검증으로 연결한다. 다른 소유자·계정·예약은 보존한다.

Vercel은 기존 24시간 안내에 따른 2026-10-24 07:37:06 KST 이후 실제 가용성을 다시 확인한다. 이 점검에서 한도를 재조회하거나 빌드 요청을 추가하지 않았다. 새 변경은 최종 프리뷰·보호 develop→data→main·exact-SHA 승인과 운영 readback을 거쳐야 한다. **이 문서는 배포 완료 증거가 아니다.**

Nightly `39457197351`은 기존 역사적 실패다. 이번 native 검사, CI 또는 향후 배포와 합쳐 통과로 표시하지 않는다.

## pstack 출처

공식 `cursor/plugins` pstack 경로의 2026-10-23 실제 확인 리비전은 `4ec7ffa173eaa70d3d9467a627baf0954645a353`, manifest 0.15.30이며 별도 stable release는 관측되지 않았다. [create-formal-verification-skill](https://github.com/cursor/plugins/blob/4ec7ffa173eaa70d3d9467a627baf0954645a353/pstack/skills/create-formal-verification-skill/SKILL.md)의 한정된 속성·반례·변형 검출·코드 동기화 원칙을 적용했다. 새 statechart 설명도 읽었으나 upstream 실행 도구나 설정은 설치하지 않았다. TLA+나 SMT solver는 실행하지 않았으며 현재 TypeScript와 DOM에 직접 연결되는 기존 Bun/Playwright를 선택했다. X 후속편의 bounded web 검색으로 원문 Part 3는 확인하지 못했으며 미발행을 뜻하지 않는다.

# Formal delta: Gemini findings and native resolution

Runtime and test source: `e2f60f7ea944776c81d538b5b042ed28ac9b04ae`. Original Gemini target: [d33](https://gemini.google.com/app/d33ca8e24b4acf9d), accepted user turn `b237ceaa27f11c3b`. Ordinary Gemini 4 Argon, highest displayed `높음 / 매우 철저함`, Deep Research OFF. The full unedited rendered response is [the retained report](gemini_4_argon_formal_delta_report.visible.txt): 22,643 UTF-8 bytes, SHA-256 `6fa6553f61a67185aecad159dd1eb93f2ced17368c01b1f63468dae0d4902069`. Native Copy/CDP timed out and clipboard stayed empty; complete rendered `innerText` was saved without editing, with a private raw HTML backup preserving mathematical/table markup. Do not treat this as the original Markdown export. The canonical full artifact passed verification and this owner released only reservation `86eb51ad0f69dcedd3c5dd33`.

| Finding | Decision and evidence | Actual correction / verification | Remaining boundary |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| GEM-F-01 | ACCEPT: the cluster animation callback directly changed `source[srcset]` independently of pool/SDK icon state. A subsequent render could compare against a stale descriptor. | `NaverMapView` sends all three cluster animation branches through `markerPool.update`; `MarkerPool.applyIcon` owns the applied snapshot for `update` and `acquire`. A browser round trip verifies current frame/count after update and reacquisition. | Chose one synchronous writer over a second asynchronous DOM writer. This removes the preload-only fast path; actual SDK pixel/flicker/performance consequences require hosted verification. No speed or no-flicker claim. |
| GEM-F-02 | ACCEPT: native Chromium reproduces a hidden outer review anchor becoming unmasked during review-only acquire. | Do not clear accessibility ownership for a successful in-place patch. SDK replacements explicitly restore the prior hidden state. Browser test checks both paths. | Synthetic SDK, synchronous root replacement; actual SDK insertion timing remains a hosted boundary. |
| GEM-F-03a | CONDITIONAL provider risk, reproduced under an admissible cloned-descriptor fixture; actual SDK clone behavior was not observed. `Bubble1 → Bubble2 → Bubble1` left Bubble2 visible. | Pool-owned `WeakMap` snapshots replace mutation of `getIcon().content`; anchors are copied. Clone-descriptor round trip now restores the requested bubble. | External direct icon writers must continue to use the pool. Actual SDK internals are unverified. |
| GEM-F-03b | ACCEPT: native browser shows existing `marginLeft:23px` disappearing after bubble replacement. | Preserve the current bubble's margin until layout recalculates it; image is retained for review-only patches. | The clamp may need recomputation for new content; existing layout pass still owns that. Pixel-level snap/flicker was not measured. |
| GEM-F-04a | ACCEPT: equal-count duplicate rows with differing effective observation timestamps resolved in opposite last-row-wins order. | Canonical video IDs before lookup; reject count or effective timestamp conflicts in either order. Alias-equivalent identical observations stay idempotent; malformed video IDs fail closed. | Timestamp validation and raw ingestion outside this builder remain separate contracts. |
| GEM-F-04b | CONDITIONAL metadata drift; a publication identifier is not a score-policy theorem. | Future builder publication ID advances to `video-views-20261023-v2`; existing frozen public assets are unchanged. | The deployed publication and source identity must be read back at release. |
| GEM-F-05 | ACCEPT as model/test gaps, not five new product failures. | The existing 48 variants now include photo DOM. Targeted checks cover anchor change, failed detach eviction/listener disposal, hidden roots, clamp and cloned descriptors. Eleven broken variants are detected. | Photo network/pixels, real SDK, unbounded/fairness/liveness remain excluded. |

Adding the photo branch exposed another **model oracle error**: unscoped `querySelector('img')` read the review photo rather than the marker image. The oracle now selects `[data-testid="marker"] img`; the product was not changed to satisfy that mistaken assertion. The retained counterexample file was captured after correcting this oracle and shows two failing tests (four observable review-boundary failures and the metric timestamp conflict), with 11 other checks passing against the original product code.

The report incorrectly counts 21 source bodies; the immutable delivery manifest and bundle contain 31. Several reported line numbers drift from the actual source; decisions bind to real functions and retained code. The report explicitly marks missing transitive modules and raw historical artifacts. Matched source samples establish those samples, not whole-tree coverage. Its strict real-number monotonicity statements must not be read as strict floating-point inequalities; bounded regressions are not all-input arithmetic proof.

## Verification and next gate

- Related native/browser checks: 48 passed, 0 failed, 1,254 assertions across 6 files; scoped ESLint passed; native/compat parity diagnostics 0, 3,382 logical inputs.
- Existing harness with new cases: 9,216 DOM transitions, 4,681 traces / 18,056 action steps, **11/11 mutants detected**, originals unchanged and restored checks passed. Full raw output is retained. This is bounded synthetic-provider evidence.
- No new build, preview or production release was requested while Vercel's retained build cap is held. Final exact-source CI, actual GPT-6 Pro ordinary reverse, final hosted PC1440/mobile390/tablet820 checks and protected release remain required.
- Historical Nightly `39457197351` remains a separate failure, not rerun or inferred from these results. Four restaurants / twelve context frames are not whole-restaurant, L1 or physical/field proof.

## EVIDENCE-01: detached artifact digest

ACCEPT. Independent readback of delivery head `9a966add6d25005f0647a3bd57301e6e3c8fc0ab` found that the old map's recorded digest-file entry matched that file, but that file's digest did **not** match the map itself. It therefore could not establish the claimed complete detached-map integrity. The updated map excludes both itself and its detached digest from its file list, writes one detached hash afterward, and independently checks that hash plus every listed file/source. Historical native results and original report bytes are preserved; this correction does not turn the old digest into a valid receipt.

+    private applyIcon(marker: PooledMarker, icon: MarkerIconLike): void {
+        const currentIcon = this.renderedIcons.get(marker) ?? marker.getIcon();
+        const currentAnchor = currentIcon?.anchor;
+        const nextAnchor = icon.anchor;

+            || (currentAnchor?.y ?? null) !== (nextAnchor?.y ?? null);
+        if (!anchorChanged && currentIcon?.content === icon.content) return;

+        const patched = !anchorChanged && typeof element?.querySelector === 'function'
+            && patchStableMarkerReviewBubble(element, currentIcon?.content, icon.content);
+        if (!patched) {
+            const wasHidden = Boolean(marker.__tzudongRetainedMarkerAriaHidden);
+            // Only SDK replacement changes the root. An in-place review patch
+            // keeps its existing accessibility owner and hidden state intact.
+            syncRetainedMarkerAccessibility(marker, true);
+            marker.setIcon(icon);
+            if (wasHidden) syncRetainedMarkerAccessibility(marker, false);
+        }
+        this.renderedIcons.set(marker, { ...icon, anchor: icon.anchor ? { ...icon.anchor } : icon.anchor });
+    }
